[\[15a.1\]]hU bau ShaT he huM hUM ho phaT haM rnams so/_/snying gar yi ge ha dang rdo rje yongs su gyur pa las rdo rje sems dpa' dkar po zhal gcig ba phyag bzhi pa phyag g.yas na rdo rje dang rnge'u chung /_g.yon pa na dril bu dang kha T+wAM ga dang thod pa 'dzin pa/_rdo rje phag mo 
[\[15a.2\]]mdog dmar mo la 'khyud pa'o/_/mgo bor yi ge hi dang 'khor lo yongs su gyur pa las rnam par snang mdzad ser po phyag g.yas na 'khor lo gzhan ni sngar bstan pa bzhin te/_gshin rje ma mdog sngon mo la 'khyud pa'o/_/spyi gtsug tu yi ge hUM dang pad+ma yongs su gyur pa las pad+ma gar gyi 
[\[15a.3\]]dbang phyug dmar po phyag g.yas na pad+ma bsnams pa rmongs byed ma mdog dkar mo'i mgrin pa nas 'dzin pa ste/_gzhan ni sngar bstan pa bzhin no/_/phrag pa gnyis su ni yi ge he dang rdo rje yongs su gyur pa las he ru ka mdog sngon po phyag g.yas na rdo rje gdengs pa bsnams pa 
[\[15a.4\]]yang dag par skyod ma mdog ljang gu la 'khyud pa'o/_/lhag ma sngar bstan pa bzhin no/_/mig gnyis su ni yi ge ho dang rin po che yongs su gyur pa las rdo rje nyi ma gser bsregs pa'i mdog can phyag g.yas na rin po che bsnams pa/_skrag byed ma nam mkha' ltar 
[\[15a.5\]]mthing skya'i mdog can la 'khyud pa ste/_lhag ma ni sngar bstan pa bzhin no/_/dpral bar yi ge hi dang sna tshogs rdo rje yongs su gyur pa las rta mchog phyag g.yas na sna tshogs rdo rje bsnams pa/_gtum mo du ba'i mdog can la 'khyud pa ste/_gzhan snga ma bzhin no/_/thams 
[\[15a.6\]]cad kyang rgyan kun gyis brgyan zhing g.yas brkyang ba'i gnas pas gnas pa ral pa'i cod pan dar gyis bcings pa'o/_/dpal he ru ka zhes bya ba'i dka' 'grel sgrub pa'i thabs kyi gleng gzhi las le'u drug pa'i rnam par bshad pa'o//_//de nas dbyangs yig ces bya ba la 
[\[15a.7\]]le'u lnga par bstan pa'i gsal byed dang dbyangs su sbyar bar bya ste/_bcu gsum nyid ces bya ba ni yi ge o'o/_/yi ge gnyis pa zhes ba ni/_rim pa ji lta ba bzhin yi ge ma'o/_/dbyangs yig bcu gcig ni e'o/_/yi ge drug pa ni ta ste rim pa 'di nyid kyis thams cad la sbyar 
[\[15b\]]
[\[15b.1\]]bar bya'o zhes so/_/dpal he ru ka zhes bya ba'i dka' 'grel sgrub pa'i thabs kyi gleng gzhi las le'u bdun pa'i rnam par bshad pa'o//_//de nas rang gi bdag nyid kyi/_/zhes bya ba ni chos thams cad gdod nas skye ba med cing bdag med pa'i rang bzhin yongs su dag cing rang bzhin 
[\[15b.2\]]dang bral bas kun du bsam pa'o/_/dpa' bo kun dang mnyam sbyor bas/_/mkha' 'gro kun gyi dra ba sdom/_/zhes bya ba ni/_snying rje chen po sprul pa'i rang bzhin te/_/rang gi bdag nyid snying po'i sngags yig ni/_/yongs rdzogs dkyil 'khor dkyil 'khor mchog_/skad 
[\[15b.3\]]cig ye shes kyis rdzogs pa/_/gru bzhi pa la sgo bzhi pa/_/rta babs bzhi yis brgyan pa ste/_/dra ba dra phyed kyis spras pa/_/dar dang me tog phreng 'phyang ba/_/phyi yi dkyil 'khor bstan par bya/_/dbus su rtsibs brgyad 'khor lo la/_/sna tshogs pad+ma bri bar bya/_/
[\[15b.4\]]zla ba nyi ma'i dbus gnas par/_/hUM gi ye shes rdzogs pa las/_/bde chen bde ba'i 'byung gnas te/_/sngo skya drag shul 'jigs pa'i 'od/_/'jigs chen ha ha zhes bzhad pas/_/'jigs pa dag kyang 'jigs par mdzad/_/zhal brgyad pa la spyan gsum pa/_/
[\[15b.5\]]bcu drug phyag gis brgyan pa ste/_/mgo bo'i phreng bas rnam par brgyan/_/sna tshogs rdo rjes mtshan pa yi/_/dbu la zla phyed ral pa 'dzin/_/phyag rgya drug gis rgyas btab sku/_/'jigs byed dang ni dus mtshan dag//g.yas brkyang ba yis yang dag mnan/_/rdo rje dril bus 
[\[15b.6\]]snyoms 'jug cing /_/phag mo'i nu ma 'dzin par byed/_/tshangs pa dral ba'i bags pa ni/_/rlon pa bzung ste sku la mnabs/_/rtse gsum mdung dang dgra sta dang /_/ral gri dang ni cang te'u ste/_/zangs kyi lcags kyu phyag g.yas na'o/_/g.yon pa de bzhin thod pa dang /_/
[\[15b.7\]]kha dang mgo dang zhags pa dang /_/tho ba sdigs mdzub phyag gzhan na/_/sngo dkar dmar ser ljang gu dang /_/du ba rdul dang thal kha mdog_/de lta bu yi zhal ldan pa/_/bcom ldan 'das kyi sku chen po/_/shes rab ye shes las rdzogs pa/_/phag mo ljang skya'i 'od 'phro 
[\[16a\]]
[\[16a.1\]]ba/_/byin pa gnyis kyis yang dag 'khyud/_/spyan gsum skra ni grol ba ste/_/thod pa'i phreng bas rnam par brgyan/_/de yi zhal dang dbyibs 'dra ba/_/dum bus mtshan pa'i ska rags can/_/zhal gyi mche ba gtsigs pa'o/_/phyogs gos chags pa'i lta ba dang /_/
[\[16a.2\]]ro smad cha ni rnam rgyas pa/_/tshangs pa'i pags pa yongs lha mo/_/skal [{]bzangs [,]bzang [}]ldan ma mda' gzhu 'dzin/_/yi ge oM gyi shar 'dab la/_/phyag mtshan mkha' 'gro ma dgod bya/_/ba yi byang gi 'dab ma la/_/rgod ma'i mkha' 'gro ma dgod bya/_/dzra yis nub 'dab snyan 
[\[16a.3\]]grags ma/_/drag shul mkha' 'gro ma dgod bya/_/he yis gser ldan ma zhes pa/_/mkha' 'gro chen mo lho phyogs dgod/_/ser po ljang gu sngon po dang /_/sngo bsangs dang ni dkar dang dmar/_/dkar dang ljang gu sngon po dang /_/de bzhin dmar dang sngo dang ljang /_/thams cad zhal 
[\[16a.4\]]gsum phyag drug pa/_/spyan gsum rnam 'gyur rab 'jigs pa/_/rgyan rnams thams cad yongs rdzogs shing /_/phyogs kyi gos can skra grol ba/_/g.yas brkyang ro yi steng gnas pa/_/bdud bzhi rab tu 'joms pa'o/_/drag po'i thod pa'i phreng ba can/_/pad+ma dang ni nu rgyas 
[\[16a.5\]]ma/_/zur gyis lta zhing bzhad pa dang /_/snying rjes chags pa'i bde dam pa/_/mda' dang gzhu ni 'dzin mdzad cing /_/zhags pa lcags kyu phyag gzhan na/_/g.yon na kha T+wAM thod pa ste/_/g.yas pas cang te'u brdung bar mdzad/_/mtshams bzhir bum par thod pa la/_/byang chub 'o mas 
[\[16a.6\]]yongs bkang ba'o/_/shar phyogs kyi rtsibs la ni yi ge he yis rtse byed ma zhes bya ba'i mkha' 'gro ma'o/_/byang gi rtsibs la ni yi ge ru yis gdu bu ma zhes bya ba'i mkha' 'gro ma'o/_/nub tu ni yi ge ru yis dung phreng can zhes bya ba'i mkha' 'gro ma'o/_/lho phyogs su ni kaM kis keng 
[\[16a.7\]]rus can ma zhes bya ba'i mkha' 'gro ma'o/_/me yi mtshams su hUM gis hUM byed ma'o/_/srin mo'i mtshams su phaT mdzad ma'o/_/rlung gi mtshams su ni mdzes can ma'o/_/dbang ldan du ni kI li kI li zhes bya ba'i ma mo'o/_/ma mo brgyad po de rnams ni/_/bcom ldan 'das kyis zhal 
[\[16b\]]
[\[16b.1\]]mdog sku/_/zhal gcig phyag ni bzhi pa la/_/thod pa kha T+wAM dril bu 'dzin/_/kun kyang cang te'u zangs gri can/_/phyogs kyi gos can g.yas brkyang gnas/_/de bzhin thod pa'i phreng ba can/_/phyag rgya lnga yis brgyan pa'o/_/ro yi steng gnas bsgom bya ste/_/drag cing rno la 'jigs rngams 
[\[16b.2\]]pa'o/_/dbang ldan mtshams su sngon byung ma/_/rlung mtshams su ni khyung mchog ma/_/srin mo'i mtshams su gsus 'phyang ma/_/me mtshams su ni ri khrod ma/_/ser po ljang gu sngon po dang /_/bzhi pa cung zad dmar po'o/_/zhal gcig pa la phyag bzhi pa/_/thod pa dang ni kha T+wAM 'dzin/_/
[\[16b.3\]]zangs gri cang te'u phyag gzhan na/_/bzhi pa la ni rdo rje'i mdung /_/phyogs kyi dregs pa'i bde dang ldan/_/gsus pa 'phyang zhing gtsigs pa ste/_/ska rags ring zhing rgyas pa'o/_/spyan gsum rnam 'gyur mi bzad pa/_/skra grol ba la 'jigs mdzad ma/_/thod pa'i phreng ba'i cod 
[\[16b.4\]]pan can/_/rgyan rnams kun gyis brgyan pa'o/_/shar phyogs kyi sgor ni/_baM gyis mi bzad drag shul can/_/zhes bya ba'i mkha' 'gro ma'o/_/lho sgor ni/_yi ge ras rol rtsed drag can/_/zhes bya ba'i mkha' 'gro ma'o/_/nub sgor ni/_swA yis rang 'od dra ba mchog_/ces bya ba'i mkha' 
[\[16b.5\]]'gro ma'o/_/byang sgor ni/_hA yis rgyal ba'i do shal mchog_/ces bya ba'i mkha' 'gro ma'o/_/de rnams zhal gsum phyag drug ma'o/_/sngo dang dmar dang dkar po ste/_/thod pa kha T+wAM ga dang ni/_/mgo dang sdigs mdzub phyag g.yon la/_/lcags kyu dang ni zangs gri dang /_/cang te'u 
[\[16b.6\]]g.yas pa'i phyag na'o/_/lho zhal dkar sngo ljang gu ste/_/thod pa kha T+wAM zangs gri 'og_/sdigs mdzub g.yon te zhags pa dang /_/lcags kyu cang te'u g.yas na'o/_/nub phyogs dmar sngo ljang gu ste/_/lcags sgrog cang te'u zangs gri dang /_/thod pa dang ni kha T+wAM ga_/mgo bo dang 
[\[16b.7\]]ni sdigs mdzub po/_/byang phyogs ljang dang dkar po dang /_/ser dang dmar 'dres dgyes pa'i zhal/_/'bebs dang cang te'u gri sdigs mdzub/_/thod pa dang ni mgo bo 'dzin/_/kun kyang skra grol drag chen mo/_/spyan gsum rnam par 'gyur ba'i zhal/_/zhal gdangs pa la 
[\[17a\]]
[\[17a.1\]]ljags 'gril ba/_/g.yas brkyang pa yis rnam par bzhugs/_/thod pa'i phreng brgyan dri ma med/_/phyogs kyi gos can dag dang ldan/_/ro dang pad+ma nyi dbus gnas/_/mi bskyod pa yi dbu rgyan can/_/zur mig blta zhing g.yo ba dang /_/snying rjes chags 
[\[17a.2\]]pa'i bde ba can/_/byang phyogs sgo la phyogs par ni/_/snying po ye shes gnas dgod bya/_/oM shrI badzra he he ru ru kaM hUM hUM phaT DA ki ni dzA la saM pa raM swA hA/_sku gsung thugs dang lhan cig tu/_/go cha drug gis brgyan pa ste/_/zhal ni rnam thar brgyad dag pa/_/phyag ni stong pa 
[\[17a.3\]]bcu drug go/_/de'i rjes la snying po nye ba'i snying po yi ge bdun pa'i bdag nyid kyi sngags chen po rdo rje mchog gi dkyil 'khor brjod par bya ste/_hUM gi yi ge yongs rdzogs las/_/rdo rje mkha' 'gro bde ba che/_/A li kA li dngos 'byung ba/_/gtso bo zhal gsum phyag 
[\[17a.4\]]drug pa/_/spyan gsum mtshan rnams kun gyis mtshan/_/dpe byad bzang po brgyad cu ldan/_/phag mo dang ni gnyis med bde/_/stong nyid snying rje 'khrigs pa yis/_/rdo rje dril bu snyoms 'jug cing /_/phyag gzhan mi yi pags pa'i gos/_/thod pa kha T+wAM ga dang ni/_/drug 
[\[17a.5\]]pa la ni mdung rtse gsum/_/thod pa'i phreng bas spras pa ste/_/rgyan rnams thams cad klubs pa yi/_/sna tshogs rdo rje zla phyed dag_/ral pa 'dzin zhing g.yas brkyang ba/_/sna tshogs pad+ma nyi dbus su/_/chung mar bcas pa'i 'jigs byed mnan/_/'dod pa'i 'bras bu kun 
[\[17a.6\]]ster ba'o/_/de'i phyi rol du 'khor lo rtsibs drug pa bris la/_/rnal 'byor ma drug der dgod par bya'o/_/rtsibs drug 'khor lo'i dbus su ni/_/myur du dngos grub sbyin byed ma/_/shar phyogs la sogs g.yon skor gyis dgod par bya ste/_yi ge phaT kyis ni dpal rol 
[\[17a.7\]]rtsed ma ste lha mo dang po'o/_/yi ge hUM gis ni rdo rje 'jigs byed ma ste gnyis pa'o/_/yi ge ha yis ni gtum drag ma ste gsum pa'o/_/yi ge ho yis ni rdo rje snang byed ma ste bzhi pa'o/_/yi ge hrIHyis ni rdo rje drag mo ste lnga pa'o/_/yi ge oM gyis ni rdo rje mkha' 'gro ma ste drug pa'o/_/lha 
[\[17b\]]
[\[17b.1\]]mo de rnams zhal gcig pa/_/phyag bzhi drag chen spyan gsum ste/_/skra grol phyogs kyi gos dang ldan/_/thams cad [{]yi dgas [,]yi dwags [}]nyi steng gnas/_/de rnams sngo dang ser ba dang /_/ljang gu dmar dang dud ka dkar/_/kun kyang cang te'u dril bsnams shing /_/mi yi pags pa gnyis 
[\[17b.2\]]kyis 'dzin/_/thod pa'i phreng ba'i dbu rgyan can/_/g.yas brkyang ba yi gnas pas bzhugs/_/yi ge drug las yang dag byung /_/dngos grub nye bar byed pa mo/_/mgon po'i sku mdog ji lta bar/_/de bzhin du ni phag mo ste/_/dbyibs dang phyag rgya de dang mtshungs/_/mi yi pags 
[\[17b.3\]]pa spangs nas su/_/lha mo mda' gzhu bsnams par mdzad/_/yan lag kun dang rtsa zhal sngo/_/g.yas dang g.yon ni ser dang ljang /_/rgyan rnams thams cad yongs rdzogs shing /_/lha mo'i pus mo gnyis 'khyud pas/_/mchog tu dga' bas myos pa mo/_/snying gar ye 
[\[17b.4\]]shes gnas bsams shing /_/sku gsung thugs kyis byin brlabs pa/_/go cha gnyis kyis rnam par brgyan/_/spro dang bsdu ba'i sbyor ba yis/_/shin tu mnyam gzhag sngags bzlas bya/_/oM hriHha ha huM hUM phaT/_yi ge drug po de rnams kyis/_/mkha' 'gro ma drug byang 
[\[17b.5\]]chub kyi/_/yan lag drug mchog bsgom par bya/_/bdun pa dbus su gyur pa ni/_/yi ge hUM mdzad he ru ka_/dran pa byang chub yan lag sogs/_/zhes pas so/_/oM d+hi d+hi d+hiM hUM phaT/_yi ge boM dang yoM dang ni/_/yi ge moM dang hriM nyid dang /_/hU~M dang phaT ces bshad pa ni/_/
[\[17b.6\]]rnal 'byor ma ni drug tu grags/_/yi ge re re yang dag gnas/_/zhes bya ba de la 'di'i cho ga rim pa bzhin dgod par bya'o/_/pad+ma 'dab ma lnga pa lte ba dang ge sar gyis 'khrigs pa bris la de'i dbus su ni yi ge baM las yang dag par byung ba'i phag mo ni/_sku mdog dmar 
[\[17b.7\]]mo 'od 'bar ma/_/zhal gsum phyag drug zhi ba ste/_/spyan gsum pa la skra grol ba/_/thod pa'i phreng ba'i dbu rgyan can/_/phyogs gos phyed krung bcas pa'i gar/_/phyag rgya drug gis btab pa'i sku/_/mgo bo'i me tog phreng ba can/_/dus mtshan 'og tu byas nas su/_/
[\[18a\]]
[\[18a.1\]]rkang pas mnan par gyur pa na/_/sdang ba kun dang lha bdud dgra/_/me ltar 'bar ba dmar chen ma/_/g.yas dang g.yon ni sngo dang ljang /_/thod pa nyid dang kha T+wAM ga_/zhags pa lcags kyu de bzhin te/_/mgo dang zangs gri 'dzin pa ni/_/dngos grub thams cad 
[\[18a.2\]]sbyin byed pa'o/_/shar phyogs la sogs pa'i lha mo rnams ni g.yon skor gyis bsam par bya ste/_yoM gis gshin rje ma'o/_/maM gyis rmongs byed ma'o/_/hriM gis yang dag skyod ma'o/_/hUM gis skrag byed ma'o/_/phaT kyis ni gtum mo ste/_de rnams thams cad zhal gcig dang /_/
[\[18a.3\]]phyag bzhi spyan gsum skra grol ba/_/phyogs kyi gos can gar gyi mchog_/phyag rgya lnga yis brgyan byas shing /_/mgo yi phreng ba'i do shal byas/_/rang rig snang ba'i dbu rgyan can/_/snying rjes 'khrug pa'i bde dam pa/_/sngo dang dkar dang ser po dang /_/ljang dang du ba skya bo ste/_/
[\[18a.4\]]thod pa dang ni kha T+wAM 'dzin/_/dril bu dang ni cang te'u gri/_/yang /_gnyis med sbyor ba snyoms zhugs pas/_/rdo rje sems sogs thams cad ni/_/'khyud la sogs pa'i rim pa bzhin/_/phyag dang mtshon cha mdog sngo gsung /_/lte ba snying ga mgrin pa mgo/_/spyi gtsug yan 
[\[18a.5\]]lag kun mtshon cha/_/zhes bya'o/_/de rjes lha mo'i snying po yi/_/sngags kyi yi ge yongs rdzogs pa/_/dkyil 'khor dkyil 'khor rnams kyi mchog_/ji ltar [{]go rims [,]go rim [}]bzhin 'byung bshad/_/yi ge so so yongs rdzogs las/_/drag po bcu dang gsum bdag nyid/_/dngos 
[\[18a.6\]]grub thams cad rab ster ba'o/_/chos kyi dbyings las 'byung ba yi/_/gzhal yas khang pa yid dga' ba/_/gru bzhi pa la sgo bzhi pa/_/rta babs bzhi yis brgyan pa ste/_/dra ba dra phyed kyis spras pa/_/dar dang me tog phreng 'phyang ba'i/_/dbus su pad+ma dmar po ni/_/
[\[18a.7\]]'dab ma brgyad pas brgyan pa'o/_/lte ba la ni phag mo ste/_/'dab mar lha mo rnams ni dgod/_/mtshams kyi sgo yi phyogs rnams su/_/lha mo rnams kyi snying po bsgom/_/phyag 'tshal ba dang rgod ma'i kha/_/dzra gi ni dang drag can ma/_/shar dang byang gi phyogs dang ni/_/nub 
[\[18b\]]
[\[18b.1\]]dang lho phyogs sgo ru'o/_/khros ma dang ni gtso mo nyid/_/kha ba ma dang gser ldan ma/_/de rnams mtshan gyi lha mo ste/_/me la sogs pa dgod par bya/_/hUM mdzad lha mo byin za'i ming /_/phaT mdzad ma dang sbas mdzad ma/_/shar dang de bzhin byang dang ni/_/nub dang lho yi 'dab 
[\[18b.2\]]lha mo/_/yi ge ha las dbus gtso mo/_/sngo zhing drag shul gzi brjid che/_/shin tu drag po bdud kun 'joms/_/mtshan rnams thams cad yongs rdzogs shing /_/rdo rje phag mo zhes bya ba/_/dngos grub thams cad rab ster ba/_/sngo dang ser dang dmar dang ljang /_/steng 
[\[18b.3\]]dkar zhi dang zhal lnga pa/_/bcu gnyis phyag dang zhal gsum ste/_/rnam 'gyur mche ba gtsigs pa ste/_/g.yas brkyang pa la legs gnas pa/_/zhabs mnan mdzad nas 'jigs byed dang /_/tsA muN+DI yang 'jigs pas brgyal/_/thod pa'i phreng can sna tshogs pa'i/_/
[\[18b.4\]]rgyan rnams kun gyis bklubs pa'o/_/gsus pa 'phyang zhing skra grol ma/_/phyogs kyi gos can rang 'od kyi/_/thabs dang bde la legs gnas pa/_/snying rje 'khrugs shing myos pa yi/_/rdo rje dril bu snyoms 'jug pa/_/thod pa kha T+wAM mdung dang ni/_/zangs gri cang te'u 
[\[18b.5\]]zhags pa dang /_/lcags kyu tshangs pa'i mgo dang ni/_/dgra sta dang ni sdigs mdzub bsnams/_/phyag gnyis mi yi pags pa yi/_/snam bu gyen du 'phyar ba'o/_/ser dang ljang dang dmar dang sngo/_/'dab ma'i lha mo rnams kyi mdog_/sngo dang ljang dang dkar dang ser/_/sgo bzhi por 
[\[18b.6\]]ni gnas pa'o/_/dmar dang dud dang sngo dang dkar/_/me sogs dbang ldan bar gnas pa'i/_/bcu gnyis de rnams zhal gcig pa/_/phyag bzhi pa la spyan gsum ste/_/rgyan rnams kun gyis bklubs pa'o/_/[{]yi dgas [,]yi dwags [}]kyi ni gdan stengs su/_/phyed krung bcas pas shin tu 
[\[18b.7\]]gnas/_/phyogs kyi gos can skra grol ba/_/nu ma gnyis 'phyang gzugs bzang ba/_/yid dga' rnam par 'gyur ba'i zhal/_/zur gyis blta zhing g.yo ba ste/_/g.yas pa rdo rje cang te'u 'dzin/_/g.yon pa kha T+wAM khrag gis ni/_/gang ba'i snod dang dril bu'o/_/'on kyang sgo'i lha 
[\[19a\]]
[\[19a.1\]]mo zhes bya ba rnams kyi gdan la/_shar phyogs kyi ni dbang po'o/_/byang phyogs kyi ni gnod sbyin no/_/nub phyogs su ni chu bdag go/_/lho'i ni gshin rje'o/_/de rnams kyi snying ga'i g.yas brkyang ba'i gnas pas gnas pa/_spyan gsum pa skra gyen du 'greng ba 
[\[19a.2\]]thod pa'i phreng ba can [(]sprul [,]sbrul [)]gyi rgyan sna tshogs kyis brgyan pa ste/_rim pa ji lta ba bzhin du lcags kyu dang zhags pa dang /_lcags sgrog dang 'bebs pa rnams gtso bo'i phyag tu'o/_/gzhan gyis rdo rje gsor ba dang thod pa dang kha T+wAM ga dang mgo bo 'dzin pa'o/_/zhal rnam par 
[\[19a.3\]]'gyur zhing gsus pa 'phyang ba kun kyang mgo ba'i phreng bas brgyan pa ste/_gcer bu pad+ma rgyas pa/_byang chub sems kyi gzi brjid bskyed cing myos pa ste/_dkyil 'khor thams cad du rang gi 'od kyis thabs dang mchog tu dga' bas gang ba'o/_/sku dang gsung dang thugs dang snying gar 
[\[19a.4\]]ye shes kyi gnas dang /_go cha gnyis rnam par brgyan la dbang bskur te mchod nas bstod la/_sngags kyi bzlas pas spro bar bya ste/_oM badzra bai ro tsa nI ye hUM hUM phaT swA hA/_sa steng na ni rin chen bzhi/_/phreng ba'i sngags ni dam pa yin/_/zhes bya ba ni/_de rnams ni yid 
[\[19a.5\]]bzhin nor bu ltar 'gro ba ma lus pa'i 'dod pa'i don sgrub par byed pa'o zhes pa'o/_/dpal he ru ka zhes bya ba'i dka' 'grel sgrub pa'i thabs kyi gleng gzhi las le'u brgyad pa'i rnam par bshad pa'o//_//las mchog [{]go rims [,]go rim [}]ji bzhin du/_/zhes bya ba la bsam gtan 
[\[19a.6\]]tsam gyis grub par 'gyur ba dang /_kha cig ni bsam gtan dang sngags kyis grub par 'gyur ba dang /_la las ni bsam gtan dang sngags dang 'khrul 'khor gyi bya bas 'grub pa ste/_rang nyid rdo rje 'dzin rgyal po/_/bka' yi 'khor lo rab sbyor bas/_/'jig rten gsum po gang 
[\[19a.7\]]cung zad/_/zhi sogs yid la 'dod tsam gyis/_/sngags mchog cho ga shes pas ni/_/de kun skad cig de la 'grub/_/klu thams cad kyi sgras ni chu bdag dang mtha' yas la sogs pa bzung ste/_sngags lan cig bzlas pa dang /_bsam gtan gyis bskul 
[\[19b\]]
[\[19b.1\]]nas bka' bkug ste/_de dag shin tu 'jigs pas myos nas rdzu 'phrul chen po dang ldan pa rnams rdzu 'phrul gyis bar snang gi nam mkha' la song ste chu'i tshogs kyi rgyun 'thor bar byed do/_/yang na chu bo dang /_rgya mtsho dang /_lteng ka dang /_khron pa dang /_rdzing bu'i 'gram rnams su 
[\[19b.2\]]rdo rje bya khyung gzi brjid chen po'i gtso bo des klu rnams la bka' bsgo bar bsgoms la/_blo bzang pos khro bas 'khrug cing bzlas par bya ste/_pad+ma 'dab ma brgyad pa nyams dga' zhing lte ba dang ge sar gyi phreng bas 'khrigs pa'i dbus su/_rdo rje chen po mche ba gtsigs pas 
[\[19b.3\]]'jigs pa dang bcas shing /_phyag brgyad pa zhal bzhi pa ste/_zhal dang po sprin gyi mdog lta bur sngo zhing khro ba dang bcas pa/_g.yas pa du ba lta bu g.yon pa sngo bsangs steng du bya khyung gi zhal shin tu dmar ba nyi ma bcu gnyis kyi 'od ltar gzi brjid rno ba mche ba gtsigs 
[\[19b.4\]]shing 'jigs par mdzad pa'o/_/g.yas pas rdo rje mchog gsor ba dang /_g.yon pas sdigs mdzub mdzad cing zhags pa dang bcas pa dang /_gzhan gnyis kyis dbang po'i pags pa'i na bza' mnabs pa/_thod pa dang ni kha T+wAM ga_/lcags kyu dang ni mdung 'dzin zhing /_/brgyad 
[\[19b.5\]]pa cang te'u ru ni 'dod/_/g.yas brkyang zhabs kyis gnon pa ni/_/chu bdag 'jigs pas brgyal bar 'gyur/_/zla phyed ral pa bcings pa la/_/sna tshogs rdo rjes nye bar brgyan/_/phyag rgya drug po sku la brgyan/_/mgo bo'i phreng bas rnam par brgyan/_/stag lpags kyi ni 
[\[19b.6\]]sham thabs can/_/mche bas ma mchu sdebs mdzad cing /_/khro bo'i lta bas gzigs mdzad cing /_/'dab ma brgyad la klu'i rgyal po brgyad bri bar bya'o/_/shar phyogs su ni mtha' yas so/_/byang phyogs su ni nor rgyas bu'o/_/nub phyogs su ni pad+ma'o/_/dbang ldan du ni pad+ma chen 
[\[19b.7\]]po'o/_/me mtshams su ni dung skyong ngo /_/srin por ni stobs kyi rgyu'o/_/rlung mtshams su ni ma dros pa ste/_dkar dang sngo dang dmar dang ljang /_/ser dang dud ka skya ljang ser/_/rin chen ldan pa'i gdengs ka mtho/_/thal mo mchog tu sbyar byas nas/_/bcom ldan 'das kyi zhal du 
[\[20a\]]
[\[20a.1\]]blta/_/'jigs pa'i sa ni bka' nod cing /_/skrag pa dang bcas blta bar bya/_/yang na phyag bcu gnyis pa'i bde ba chen po bsgom par bya ste/_sngon du bstan pa bzhin te/_chu bdag chung ma bcas pa mdog dkar po mnan pa'o/_/klu rnams dang chu bdag ni snying 
[\[20a.2\]]gar hUM nAM hUM bri'o/_/bris sku la dmigs shing shar du bltas pas rtsa ba'i sngags bzlas par bya ste/_'bum gyis na mngon du gyur nas/_rdo rje khro bo bya khyung zhes bya ba'i gtso bo chen po'i bka' tsam gyis char 'bebs par 'gyur ro/_/oM aM a nan+tA ya hUM nA hUM swA hA/_oM bAM bA su ki/_oM taM tak+Sha ka_/
[\[20a.3\]]oM paM pad+ma/_oM maM ma hA pad+ma/_oM saM saM kha pA la/_oM kaM karko Ta ma/_oM aM a na ba ta ba ta/_oM baM ba ru Na ya nA gA d+hi pa ta ye nA gA nAM baR+Sha pa ya hUM nAM hUM swA hA/_zhes bya ba ni thams cad blta bar bya'o/_/khro bo chen po sgom pas thams cad khri phrag tu bzlas pa dbul lo/_/mi smra bar gyur cing 
[\[20a.4\]]shin tu mnyam par gzhag pas tshangs par spyod cing 'o ma dang /_sA lu'i 'bras chan bza' bar bya ste/_klu'i gtor ma dang ldan zhing /_'o mas khrus byas la 'o ma'i sbyin sreg dbul bar bya ste/_'o ma can gyi shing las skyes pa'i yam shing mtho gang pa dang lhan cig bsreg par byas la bum pa 
[\[20a.5\]]'o mas bkang ste 'o ma dang bcas pa'i gtor ma phyogs bcur rab tu sbyin no/_/de lta bu'i cho gas mchod la/_dus bzhir stong phrag bzlas par bya ste/_zhag bdun gyis nges par sa gzhi thams cad du char 'bab par 'gyur ro/_/yang na 'dir chu dang yungs kar dngul gyi snod du blugs 
[\[20a.6\]]la lan nyi shu rtsa gcig bzlas so/_/sangs rgyas kyi gzugs brnyan gdung dang bcas pa la/_dri chu dang /_'o mar bsres pas khrus byas la chu 'gram gnyis kyi sa gcig tu byas nas klu'i gzugs bya ste/_mgo dgu pa nub tu bltas ba gzhag go/_/de nas khrus byas la mchod de/_
[\[20a.7\]]de dang khrus kyi chur bcas pa chu klung du gtor bar bya'o/_/de ma thag tu klu rnams mgu nas 'dzam bu'i gling thams cad du char rgyun mi 'chad pa 'bebs par 'gyur/_sa gzhi 'bru dang ldan par 'gyur zhing /_sems can thams cad bde ba'i reg pa'i gnas pas gnas par 'gyur ro/_/de nas 
[\[20b\]]
[\[20b.1\]]'khrul 'khor gyi rab tu sbyor bar bya ste/_sngags kyi 'khrul 'khor las byung ba/_/gdengs ka'i dbang phyug 'khor lo'i ming /_/mchog tu rmad byung brtag pa yang /_/thos pa tsam gyis skrag byed na/_/ming dang bcas par bris pa yis/_/gdengs ka thams cad smos ci dgos/_/gro ga 
[\[20b.2\]]'am ni ras dkar la/_/'dab brgyad pad+ma bri bar bya/_/'khor lo gsum po rim ji bzhin/_/rdo rje pad+ma 'khor phreng bskor/_/dug dang lan tsha ske tshe dang /_/tsha ba gsum dang dur khrod sol/_/sna tshogs g.yon gyi skor phyogs su/_/shin tu mnyam par gzhag pa yis/_/shar la 
[\[20b.3\]]sogs pa'i phyogs dang ni/_/dbang ldan phyogs kyi mtshams nas brtsam/_/ka ra ka ra la sogs pa'i/_/sngags 'di dag nas brtsams nas ni/_/rab gtum mo la sogs pa dang /_/brtson chen mtha' mar rab tu gnas/_/rang ming bcas par bri bya ste/_/huM hUM phaT dang sbyar 
[\[20b.4\]]bar bya/_/oM ni thams cad gsal byed cing /_/grub sbyin 'dod pa thams cad gsal/_/'khor lo gsum gyis yongs bskor ba'i/_/rtsibs mchan mu khyud klu brgyad po/_/mtha' yas la sogs bri bar bya/_/'dab brgyad lte ba'i dbus su ni/_/hUM nAM hUM sarba nA ga baR+Sha 
[\[20b.5\]]pa ya shI g+h+raM phaT/_dkyil 'khor gyi ni phyi rol du/_/rin chen dang ldan gdengs gsum mtho'i/_/mjug ma phan tshun skye dkris bskor/_/de rnams kyi ni gdengs ka la/_/hUM nAM hUM ni rnam dgod bya/_/grog mo'i mkhar gyi sa dang ni/_/ba yi rnam lngar bcas pa yis/_/mtho gang 
[\[20b.6\]]tsam pa'i nor rgyas kyi/_/gzugs brnyan byas la de yis ni/_/snying gar 'khor lo gzhug bya ste/_/dgug sogs byas nas yang dag mchod/_/ka ra bI ra'i lcug bkab ste/_/bar chad med par chus khrus bya/_/lan cig bzlas pa'i gu gul gyis/_/bdug pa rgyun mi 
[\[20b.7\]]'chad par sbyar/_/gal te 'bab par ma gyur na/_/de ni nyi ma bdun pa la/_/dum bu dum bur byas nas su/_/bum pa'i nang du gzhug byas la/_/chu bo chen por bskur bar bya/_/de yis klu kun pham nas ni/_/'jigs dang bcas pas brgyal 'gyur te/_/chu yi tshogs ni 'bebs 
[\[21a\]]
[\[21a.1\]]par byed/_/lag pa g.yon pa la 'khor lo yid kyis bsams la/_ming dngos su bris nas lan cig bzlas pas ni dus su sbrul gyis zin pa slong bar byed do/_/lag pa bstan pa tsam gyis lha thams cad dang gnod sbyin thams cad dang srin po la sogs 
[\[21a.2\]]pa'i gdon gyis gzir ba las grol bar 'gyur ro/_/yang na/_kham phor so ni ma btang bar/_/dug la sogs pas bris byas pa/_/dkyil 'khor gsum po chu dag gis/_/'dab brgyad pad+mas mtshan pa yi/_/dkyil 'khor dam pa bris byas la/_/lte bar yi ge baM bris 
[\[21a.3\]]nas/_/hUM gnyis kyis ni gzir bar bya/_/'dab brgyad la ni de bzhin du/_/klu brgyad snying po gzir ba bri/_/de nas sngags 'di gdab par bgyi/_/mtsho skyes kun du bskur bar bya/_/oM badzra kro d+ha ma hA ba laM/_ha na ha na pa tsa pa tsa bi d+h+wan+sa ya/_u ts+tsha Ta ya/_sarba Ni mA ra ya 
[\[21a.4\]]hUM phaT/_de nas dgug pa la sogs pa byas nas yang dag par mchod de/_kha sbyar la klu'i sar gzhug ste/_gu gul dang sardza ra dang sgog skya bsres pa'i bdug pas bdug cing sngags bzlas na char 'bab po/_/zlog par byed do zhes bya ba ni bka' yi 'khor lo rab sbyor 
[\[21a.5\]]bas so/_/yang na rdo rje 'dzin pa'i spyi bor phul ba'i me tog blangs la rtsa ba'i sngags lan bdun btab ste srin lag la bdugs na 'dis nam mkha' la char bzlog par 'gyur ro/_/rgya mtsho skems par byed do zhes bya ba ni/_ji ltar gsungs pa'i cho ga dang /_sgom pa rgya 
[\[21a.6\]]mtshor bung ba'i sngags pas shin tu khros pa'i gzi brjid chen pos skams par 'dir gdon mi za'o/_/kha dang kha la yongs su bzlas nas bsnun na chu bo rgyun las bzlog go/_/thog zlog par byed do zhes bya ba ni sdigs mdzub tsam gyis so/_/bzlas brjod 'bum byas pas 
[\[21a.7\]]sa g.yo bar byed ces bya ba ni/_g.yas brkyang sbyor bas gar mdzad bzhin pa'i dga' ba dang bcas spro ba dang bcas pas mkha' 'gro ma'i 'khor lo rab tu mgu bar byed do/_/shing la 'dzegs te zhes bya ba la sogs pa ni gang gi tshe sgrub pa po grub par 'gyur ba de'i tshe thams cad 
[\[21b\]]
[\[21b.1\]]byed do zhes bya ba'o/_/gzugs brgya stong du ma zhes bya ba la sogs pa ni/_phyag rgya chen po'i go 'phang la gnas pas 'jig rten 'dir gang grub pa'i sangs rgyas de la lta ci smos so/_/snying rje chen po'i 'jug pa'i smon lam grub pa rnams ni dus rtag tu sems can ji ltar 'dul 
[\[21b.2\]]ba'i dbang gis de dang de'i gzugs 'dzin par 'gyur ro/_/gzhan yang brtson 'grus dman gyur pa/_/gang tshe sgrub po dngos grub 'dod/_/de tshe sngags dang rgyud kyis 'grub/_/sngags ni yid skyob ces grags pa/_/rgyud sman bzang po thabs chen thob/_/su la dngos grub 
[\[21b.3\]]grub mi 'gyur/_/ma lus gzugs su sgyur bar byed/_/rang bzhin bsod nams dman pas kyang /_/tshogs pa rnams ni rnyed 'gyur na/_/rnam pa sna tshogs la sogs pa'i/_/sems can rnams ni chags bya'i phyir/_/bya ba rnams ni skyed par byed/_/dang po lha'i bdag nyid du 
[\[21b.4\]]gyur par byas la/_phyi nas sa bon gyi dngos po grub ste/_ji lta bu'i sa bon la sogs pa yin zhe na shing gi tshogs kyis te/_e raN+Da dang smyo byed la sogs pa bshad pa dang de bzhin du sa bon rnams so/_/de dag la sogs pa yongs su smin pa rnams cho ga bzhin du sgrub par 
[\[21b.5\]]byed de/_dkar po ma yin pa'i 'byung po'i nyi ma la rigs ngan gyi me tog gis bsgo zhing bdud rtsi lngas kyang mnyes la nyin mtshan de la skams te/_khyi nag po'i thod par dur khrod kyi sas bkang la/_mar ngo'i tshes brgyad dam bcu bzhi la ji ltar bstan pa'i ma mo'i gnas su bskyed nas 
[\[21b.6\]]sgrub pa po'i sa bon blangs la/_sngags pas he ru ka dang bla ma mgon po khyad par du mchod pa byas la dur khrod du gtor mas mchod de/_mngon par bsngags la gang gi gzugs su bsams nas mgrin par bcings nas de'i gzugs su bsgyur te bkrol na slar yang rang gi gzugs 
[\[21b.7\]]su 'gyur ro/_/yang gser shing gi sa bon blangs la zla ba mar ngo la sbrul nag po'i zhag gis byugs la/_mar ngo'i tshes brgyad dang /_bcu bzhi la khwa nag po'i thod par ji skad bstan pa'i gnas su mnyam par gzhag pas bskyed de/_smin ma'i sa bon blangs nas cho ga bzhin bya 
[\[22a\]]
[\[22a.1\]]ste/_gang yin pa'i gzugs su bsams nas mgrin pa bcings na de'i mod la sbed par 'gyur ro/_/dang por re zhig cho ga shes pas bram ze mchog gi rigs las byung ba'i gsal shing ngam mtshon gyis brgyab pa yang rung ste de'i thod pa blangs la/_grog mkhar 
[\[22a.2\]]gyi sas bkang ste dgongs mo'i dus su dur khrod dam lam gyi bzhi mdo dang sum mdo dang chu bo'i 'gram rnams su sa bon bcug la chus gtor ba dang gtor ma sbyin pa dang nyi ma re re bsrung bar bya ste/_dpal he ru ka'i gzugs phyag rgya dang rgyan kun gyis brgyan pa/_kha T+wAM ga dang thod pa 
[\[22a.3\]]lag pa g.yon pas 'dzin cing /_lag pa g.yas pas rnge'u chung 'dzin pa'i spyod pa'i gzugs kyi brtul zhugs kyis bsrung bar bsams na sha za la sogs pa rnams kyis tshugs par mi 'gyur la gzhan du na mngon par 'gyur ro/_/sman bzang po rdzogs par bya ba la ni tshes dang 
[\[22a.4\]]skar ma dang gza' bzang po la gzugs pa dang blang bar bya ste/_gang dang 'dra ba'i gzugs su byed par 'dod pa'i sngags pa mnyam par gzhag par byed de/_gang dang gang gi khrag gis 'bras bu rnams la mang du gtor zhing de'i sha la sogs pas byugs la de'i zhag gis 
[\[22a.5\]]kyang mnye bar bya ste/_de'i thod pa ji ltar bstan pa de bzhin bskyed cing /_de'i zhag dang chu dang sha dang khrag gis gtor ma dang bdug pa dang byug pa la sogs pa nan tan du yang dang yang du byas la/_smin pa'i dus blang bar bya ste/_gcer bu spyi gtsug grol bas bu mo'i lag 
[\[22a.6\]]pa g.yon par thod pa bzhag cing /_ci nas kyang 'bras bu sa la ma lhung bar gzung ngo /_/grub pa'i sa bon de bsgrims te blangs la cha gsum du byas la gcig ni bla ma rdo rje 'chang la dbul lo/_/gnyis pa ni de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad dang dpa' bo dang rnal 'byor ma 
[\[22a.7\]]rnams la dbul lo/_/gsum pa ni bdag nyid kyis bzung la/_khwa'i gdong pa la sogs pa'i rnal 'byor 'byung po'i ma mo'i dkyil 'khor gyi dbus su gnas te khar bcug na rnal 'byor pa sna tshogs kyi rnam pa dang mtshungs par snang ste/_cho ga ji lta ba bzhin du nges par 
[\[22b\]]
[\[22b.1\]]de 'dra ba'i gzugs su mthong bar 'gyur ro/_/sbyor ba'i nus pa skyes bu thams cad du mi snang bar 'grub par 'gyur te/_sa bon gyi dam tshig 'grub pa'i cha tsam yang 'ug pa la sogs pa rnams kyis mi mthong na sa la skyes pa rnams kyis lta smos kyang ci dgos te/_blo dang ldan 
[\[22b.2\]]pas ji srid 'dod pa'i bar du mnyam par gzhag na lha'i bu mo rnams dang lhan cig tu rtse bar 'gyur ro/_/'dod na kha nas bton na mthong zhing mi 'dod na mi mthong bar 'gyur te/_mi snang ba'i dbang phyug tu 'dod par 'gyur ro/_/khang [{]bzangs [,]bzang [}]dang shing gi rtse mo la sogs phye mar byed 
[\[22b.3\]]do zhes bya ba ni/_/sngags kyi rgyal pos me 'bar ba'i/_sta gri bzhin du gtub par bya'o/_/lag pa g.yon pa zla ba'i 'od ltar dri ma med pa'i 'khor lo 'di bkod na gnas pa dang rgyu ba dang yan lag las skyes pa dang sbyar ba las byung ba'i dug rnams lag pa'i mthil bstan pa tsam gyis 
[\[22b.4\]]bdud rtsir byed cing zla ba'i 'od ltar dri ma med pa'i 'od zer 'bar bas dug sna tshogs kyis brgyal ba yang slong bar byed do/_/bdud rtsi bcud kyis len gsungs te/_bdud rtsi za ba la sogs pa'o/_/dug tu byed do zhes bya ba ni 'bad pa med par gang du sngags nag por byas pa nyid kyis 
[\[22b.5\]]so/_/yang na ku ba khan de'i sa bon blangs la sbrul nag po'i zhag gis byugs te/_zla ba mar ngo'i gza' ma rungs pa la dgongs ka'i dus su sbrul nag po'i thod par bzhi mdo'i sas bkang la btsugs nas skyes bu'i khu ba'i thig le byas nas gang la bltas pa'i bza' ba dang bca' ba 
[\[22b.6\]]dang btung ba la sogs pa rnams kha zhing sngags dang bcas pa ni dug tu 'gyur la 'dod na rnal mar 'gyur ro/_/zla ba zhes bya ba chang ngo /_/'o ma zhes bya ba 'o ma'i btung ba'o/_/zla ba'i 'od ltar dri ma med pa'i bsam gtan tsam gyis so/_/yang na shing arka dkar po dang me sbar 
[\[22b.7\]]la pad+ma'i snal ma phra mo phye mar byas te/_'o ma dang lan mang du btags te sngags kyi sbyin sreg brgya rtsa brgyad kyi bar du dbul nas de'i thal ba blangs la tsing ka'i khu bas sbrus te/_grib ma la lan bdun gyi bar du bskam par bya'o/_/de nas mchod pa'i dus su rnal 'byor pa rnams kyi 
[\[23a\]]
[\[23a.1\]]sor mo'i nang bkang la chang gi snod kyi nang du thal ba blugs la 'o ma dang kun da dang /_zla ba ltar dri ma med par 'gyur ro/_/rtog pas rmongs pa'i sems can rnams la las 'di spyad par bya'o/_/ji ltar ku su ma'i me tog bskams pa'i phye ma la dzam bi ri 'bras bu'i khu bas 
[\[23a.2\]]lan mang du sbrus la ba'i gcin gyis sbangs nas pad+ma'i snod du blugs pas ni 'o ma dang chang dang 'dra bar byed do/_/tsher ma dang bcas pa'i ka rany+dza'i 'bras bu'i phye ma dang nA ri'i pags pa dang bcas pas/_ltag pa la sogs pa la byugs na dri 'phrog par byed do/_/de ltar 
[\[23a.3\]]me tog dang shing dang 'bras bu la sogs pa thams cad kyi dri 'phro'o/_/me tog sna tshogs pa la phan tshun du bdug pa la sogs pas bdugs pa'am bsregs na/_gang du 'dod pa dri bro bar byed do/_/nam mkha' lding gi gdon gyis 'gro ba 'byin par byed cing 
[\[23a.4\]]zhes bya ba la sogs pa la 'khor lo de nyid klu rnams spangs shing zla bas nye bar brgyan pa la lha'i bu mo dang skyes pa gnyis kyi ming dang spel la skar ma rgyal gyi dus su go ro tsa nas bris la mchod pa chen po byas te bzung la bdag nyid dpal he ru ka'i bdag nyid du sbyor zhing /_
[\[23a.5\]]rang gi rig ma ni rdo rje phag mos yongs su brtags pa gnyis su med pa'i rnal 'byor gyi ting nge 'dzin la gnas nas mtshan thog thag rig pa'i rgyal po bzlas pa byas na rang nyid 'ongs te 'dus pa ni bud med dang 'dus pa zhes bya'o/_/rig ma las rdul rang byung ste bud med rnams 
[\[23a.6\]]kyang 'dus par 'gyur ro/_/nam mkha' lding gi gdong gis 'gro ba zhes bya ba ni/_go bzlog 'dug stangs kyis shin tu rgyas pa'i pad+ma'i nang du chud pa'i khrag dang bcas pa'i rtsa kha bye ba las 'byin par byed pa ni de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi rang bzhin gyi rigs su 
[\[23a.7\]]'gyur bas mi mchog ste dpal dang ldan zhing mtshan dri ma med par 'gyur ro/_/de'i lag mthil na yod pa'i dpal gang gis kyang 'phrogs mi nus te/_'byung po thams cad kyi mchod gnas su 'gyur bas 'khor los sgyur ba dang mnyam par dpal dang ldan zhing tshe dang nad med par phun 
[\[23b\]]
[\[23b.1\]]sum tshogs pas mtshan pa'o/_/dran pa tsam gyis gsod par byed do zhes bya ba la 'khor lo de nyid nyi ma'i dkyil 'khor gyi nang du chud par dug dang lan tshwa dang ske tshes bris la dur khrod kyi thal ba dang sol bas gzugs brnyan byas la snying gar bcug la bsgrub bya dran bzhin du bkug ste bcug 
[\[23b.2\]]la dur khrod du sbas nas mi'i rus pa'i phur bus snying gar btab nas chu tshan blugs la khro bas sngags bzlas na nyi ma bdun gyis 'chi bar 'gyur ro/_/gtum mo sbar ba 'bar ba'i phreng ba'i ting nge 'dzin la gnas nas lho phyogs kyi rlung gis brdabs shing bus na gang yin pa der me skyed par 
[\[23b.3\]]byed do zhes pa'o/_/rtse gcig pa'i rdo rje gsor zhing sngags bzlas pas ni sa 'og pa rnams 'joms par byed do/_/lag pa'i mthil du mngon par bsngags nas rlung gi rjes su bsnun na gang du 'dod pa der ser ba 'bebs par 'gyur ro/_/dran pa tsam gyis glog skyed par byed do zhes bya 
[\[23b.4\]]ba la sogs pa ni go sla'o/_/dur khrod kyi sol ba zhes bya ba la sogs pa la zla ba mar ngo'i tshes brgyad dam bcu bzhi la ro bsregs pa'i me la shing arka bsregs la da du ra'i khu bas me bsad nas sol ba des dug dang lan tshwar bcas pas dur khrod ras la nyi ma'i dbus su chud pa'i 
[\[23b.5\]]'khor lo bris nas dur khrod kyi thal bas gzugs brnyan byas te/_de'i snying gar ming dang spel ba bcug la dgug pa la sogs pa byas la gu gul chen pos bdugs nas gang gi ming nas bzung ste/_sngags kyis lan cig bzlas te bsnun na de'i mod la ltung bar 'gyur ro/_/
[\[23b.6\]]'khor lo de nyid sa kwa ta ka'i smyu gus sha za'i snying gar cho ga bzhin du bris la ji skad gsungs pa'i thal [(]bsngags [,]ba sngags [)]dang bcas pas gang gi ming nas smos te bsnun na de sha zas gzung bar 'gyur ro/_/dur khrod kyi sol ba zhes bya ba la gong du bstan pa'i sol bas 'byung po'i nyin zhag 
[\[23b.7\]]gi mtshan mo la spang leb bam rtsig pa'i ngos sam dben pa'i gnas gang yang rung bar gza' rnams kyi phyag mtshan gyi dbus su 'khor lo la bsgrub bya'i ming du spel ba [(]bres [,]bris [)]nas gu gul chen pos bdugs na de gzas bzung bar 'gyur ro/_/slar gso ba la ni de dag thams cad 
[\[24a\]]
[\[24a.1\]]la yang nan tan du 'o mas bkru zhing zhi ba'i yid kyis bzlas par bya'o/_/a ti muk+ta'i lcug ma zhes bya ba la sogs pa la tsan+dan dmar po'i rdul las bsgrub bya'i gzugs brnyan byas la dur khrod kyi ras la ro'i sen rtsi dang ro tsa na las sngon du gsungs pa'i 'khor 
[\[24a.2\]]lo zla ba'i dkyil 'khor la bris te snying gar bcug la dri'i chus khrus byas nas dgug pa la sogs pa bya zhing /_a ti muk+ta'i lcug ma la/_bsgrub bya'i ming dang spel ba'i sngags lan bdun bzlas nas gang la bsnun na dga' ba chen po'i mdog dmar zhing gcer bu skra grol ba 
[\[24a.3\]]myos shing byang chub kyi sems rgyun du 'bab pa rnga mo la zhon pa dbugs kyi zhags pas bkug ste/_rkang pa dag gi mdun du [(]'gyil [,]'gyel [)]bar bsam mo/_/nyi ma bdun yongs su rdzogs pas rlung gi shugs kyis 'ong bar 'gyur ro/_/mi ro'i thod tshal la ldong ros dang /_yung bas gang 
[\[24a.4\]]gi ming dang spel ba'i 'khor lo nyi ma'i dkyil 'khor gyi dbus su bris nas dur khrod dam lam gyi bzhi mdor khru gang tsam du brkos te/_kha sbub tu sbas la gseg ma la lan bdun bzlas nas bsnun te/_de ji srid 'tsho'i bar du bcings par 'gyur ro/_/gang gi tshe bgrod 'dod 
[\[24a.5\]]pa de'i tshe yang tshub mas khyer ba'i shing lo la mchi ma dang khwa'i khrag dang bya rog gi sgro'i rtsa ba la smyu gus ming dang bcas par bris nas gyen du ldang ba'i rlung la bskur na gang du 'dod pa der 'gro bar 'gyur ro zhes so/_/rengs pa gso ba la ni phyung nas bkru bar bya'o/_/ra nag po'i yan 
[\[24a.6\]]lag lnga dang til mar chen po dang gu gul chen po rnams cha bsnyams la chang gis sbrus te mchod pa'i dus su lan cig bzlas te bdug par sbyar na de bsnams pa tsam gyi mod la 'bebs par 'gyur ro/_/yang dur khrod kyi ras la shing a sho ka'i smyu gus klu rnams spangs pa'i 
[\[24a.7\]]'khor lo de nyid la bris la yi ge e'i dbus su rak+Sha zhes bya ba'i ming dang spel bar byas nas yang dag par mchod cing de khyim gyi phyogs gcig tu bzhag na lus srul po thams cad bcings par 'gyur ro/_/'khor lo de nyid du dur khrod kyi ras la bsgrub bya'i ming dang spel te 
[\[24b\]]
[\[24b.1\]]bris nas bud med kyi ro bsregs pa'i thal pa dang tsan+dan dmar po'i rdul du bsres pas bsgrub bya'i gzugs brnyan byas la dgug pa la sogs pa byas nas snying gar bcug nas yang dag par mchod de ka ra bI ra'i lcug ma bsgrub bya'i lag pa'i tshad tsam bud med ro bsregs pa'i 
[\[24b.2\]]'bar ba'i me la bskyur la ji srid ma tshig par de myur du blangs te rig pa'i rgyal po lan cig bzlas nas gang zhig dran bzhin bskor na de 'gugs par byed/_ltor bsnun na mngal 'pho bar 'gyur ro/_/gzhan yang rkang pa brgyad pa yi/_/sngags kyi las mchog bshad par bya/_/
[\[24b.3\]]nam mkha'i dbyings kyi dbus su ni/_/zla ba'i dkyil 'khor bsam par bya/_/sku gsung thugs kyi 'khor lo yi/_/dbus su bde chen 'khor lo bri/_/'dab brgyad pad+ma ge sar ldan/_/rdo rje pad+ma 'khor lo yi/_/phreng bas gsum bskor dbus su ni/_/sna tshogs rdo rje'i phreng bskor 
[\[24b.4\]]nas/_/'od zer tshogs ni [(]spo [,]spro [)]bya ste/_/'on kyang dkar dmar sngo rnam pa/_/las kyi 'od zer gyis brgyan pa/_/dbus su dpa' bo'i snying po dang /_/ji ltar bstan zhing bsgrub bya'i ming /_/de lta bu yi cho gas bri/_/'di ni srung ba chen po ste/_/srung ba kun gyi dam pa yin/_/rims 
[\[24b.5\]]dang lto ba na ba dang /_/dug gi nad dang mkha' 'gro nye bar 'tshe ba dang /_gza'i gnod pa dang /_bdud dang /_log 'dren dang /_gang dag lus srul po la sogs pa dag grib ma la yang 'da' bar mi nus na de'i lus la lta ci smos/_/zla gzugs 'dra ba'i yi ge blta/_/phyogs bcur 
[\[24b.6\]]'byer bar byed pa yin/_/dkar po yis ni zhi bar 'gyur/_/nag pos skad cig gsod par 'gyur/_/dmar pos dbang du byed 'gyur te/_/de nyid kyis ni nges par 'gugs/_/ser po yis ni rengs pa dang /_/dpung tshogs bzhon par bcas pas gnon/_/rnal 'byor ma yi 
[\[24b.7\]]snying po mchog_/gsang ba las kyang shin tu gsang /_/sngags kyi de nyid rab sbas pa/_/he ru ka dpal rdo rje che/_/rgyud ni gang gis mi shes shing /_/bla ma che la gus med par/_/sngags kyi dngos grub 'bras 'dod dang /_/rnal 'byor pa ni rnal 'byor 'dod/_/gsang sngags 
[\[25a\]]
[\[25a.1\]]'di ni rnam spangs pas/_/de ni 'bras bu thob mi 'gyur/_/dper na la la 'bras 'dod pas/_/phub ma rdung bar byed dang mtshungs/_/de ni ngal ba don med de/_/'bras bu thob par mi 'gyur ro/_/de phyir mchog 'dod gang yin pas/_/'bad de rdo rje slob 
[\[25a.2\]]dpon gzung /_/gsang sngags 'di ni 'dod spyod pa'i/_/byin gyi rlabs kyis 'bras bu ster/_/zhes so/_/dpal he ru ka zhes bya ba'i dka' 'grel sgrub pa'i thabs kyi gleng gzhi las le'u dgu pa'i rnam par bshad pa'o//_//de nas sku gsum bshad bya ste/_/zhes bya 
[\[25a.3\]]ba la sku gsum gyi sgras ni chos dang longs spyod rdzogs pa dang sprul pa rnams so/_/de la sku dang gsung gi gsang ba zhes bya ba bsod nams kyi rgyu mthun pa'i mtshan nyid ni/_de bzhin gshegs pa'i longs spyod rdzogs pa dang sprul pa'i mtshan nyid de/_kun rdzob dang don dam 
[\[25a.4\]]pa'i mtshan nyid do/_/gang zhig rten cing 'brel par 'byung bar zhugs shing khongs su chud pas chad pa med pa rtag pa med pa/_yid kyi rnam par shes pa'i lam nas 'jug pa'i bsam gtan goms shing byang bas mi dmigs shing kun nas nyon mongs pa med pa rnam par 
[\[25a.5\]]byang ba med pa stong pa ma yin zhing lus kyi yul ma yin pa dag gi spyod yul las 'das pa yid kyi rang bzhin gyi yul ma yin pa de ni de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi nam mkha'i khams dang mnyam par 'jug pa chos kyi sku'o/_/de ltar de bzhin gshegs pa rnams kyi don 
[\[25a.6\]]dam pa'i sgo nas sku gsum po blta bar bya'o/_/de rnams kyi sgrub pa bshad par bya ste/_gang zhe na/_he ru ka dpal gnyis med sbyor/_/zhes bya ba ste gzhal yas khang gi dbus su yi ge b+h+rUM yongs su gyur pa las 'khor lo rtsibs brgyad pa de'i dbus su sna tshogs pad+ma 'dab ma 
[\[25a.7\]]brgyad pa'i steng du zla ba'i dkyil 'khor de'i dbus su A li kA li yongs su gyur pa las rdo rje sems dpa' sku mdog dkar po zhal gcig phyag bzhi pa phyag g.yas na rdo rje dang rnge'u chung /_g.yon na thod pa dang kha T+wAM ga dang dril bu 'dzin pa/_phag mo mdog dmar mo la 'khyud pa 
[\[25b\]]
[\[25b.1\]]g.yas brkyang ba'i gnas pas gnas pa ste/_bsrub bya srubs pa'i sbyor bas 'dod chags kyi me 'bar bas zhu bar gyur par blta'o/_/de'i 'od zer las nges par 'byung ba'i rnal 'byor ma rnams kyi rdo rje'i glus yang dag par bskul bar bsams te/_oM hUM a ma hA su kha a la la la ho/_
[\[25b.2\]]ma hA su kha ra tsa tas+t+waM/_su ra ta su kha bi yog su ra to a ho ma hA su khaM su kha ma nu sid+d+hi ho/_su ra ta sa ma ya sau khya ba yaM/_oM hUM a ho a ho b+ha ga bAn ma hA su khaM/_ut+ta ra sarbo tha mo sau khya ma ya hUM a la la la ho/_staM ba ra ma su kha ma len du po bo tiM ma dza la su khaM a la la la ho/_bi ti 
[\[25b.3\]]swa bya ma ya/_sgra brnyan dang 'dra ba'i glus bskul ma thag tu de'i ngo byas nas bzhengs te/_rig rnams kyi bdag po chos kyi sku'i rang bzhin gyis 'od gsal ba dpal thugs kyi mkha' 'gro bde ba chen po mdog sngo zhing gzi brjid chen po nyi ma bye ba ltar gsal zhing /_khro bo'i lta 
[\[25b.4\]]ba dang lo bcu drug lon pa'i rnam pa lta bu mtshan thams cad yongs su rdzogs pa dpe byad thams cad kyis yang dag par brgyan zhing /_zhal bzhi pa phyag bcu gnyis pa/_g.yas brkyang ba'i gnas pas bzhugs shing /_spyan gsum pa zhal mche ba rnam par gtsigs pa ste/_
[\[25b.5\]]zhal g.yon pa dang g.yas pa dkar zhing ser ba nub phyogs kyi zhal dmar po'o/_/phyag dang po gnyis rdo rje dang dril bu yang dag par ldan pa gzhan gyis khyab 'jug gi mgo bo bzung nas khyab 'jug gi pags pa bsnams pa ste/_phyag lhag ma rnams kyis dgra sta dang mdung rtse 
[\[25b.6\]]gsum dang zangs gri dang cang te'u rnams ni phyag g.yas na'o/_/g.yon pas ni zhags pa dang thod pa dang kha T+wAM ga dang sdigs mdzub bo/_/thod pa'i phreng bas dpa' bo dbu bcings shing /_spyi bor mtho ba zla ba phyed pas ral pa bcings te/_sna tshogs rdo rjes mtshan pa bsnams 
[\[25b.7\]]pa stag gi pags pa mnabs shing /_rgyan thams cad kyis klubs pa rdo rje mkha' 'gro ma la 'khyud pa ste/_de ni zhal gcig_/phyag gnyis pa spyan gsum pa gcer bu mdog sngo ba ste/_dum bus mtshan pa'i ska rags can rgyan thams cad yongs su rdzogs shing phyag 
[\[26a\]]
[\[26a.1\]]g.yon pas 'khyud cing thod pa thogs pa g.yas pa rdo rje gdengs pa dang sdigs mdzub mdzad pa'o/_/'dab ma rnams la ni mkha' 'gro ma la sogs pa'o/_/'khor lo rtsibs brgyad la ni rab tu gtum mo la sogs pa dang /_khwa'i gdong pa la sogs pa nas gshin 
[\[26a.2\]]rje 'joms ma'i mthar thug pa 'di ni rnal 'byor ma nyi shu ste zhal gcig pa phyag bzhi pa la dkar po dang ser po dang dmar po dang ljang gu ni mkha' 'gro ma la sogs pa'o/_/mtshams rnams su ni dum bu'i thod pas mtshan pa'o/_/yang dkar po dang ser po dang dmar po dang ljang gu 
[\[26a.3\]]rnams ni g.yon skor gyis phyogs kyis so/_/sngo skya dang ser po dang ljang gu dang ser po ni g.yas skor gyis mtshams na gnas pa'o/_/thod pa'i dum bu can dang rab tu gtum mo la sogs pa'i dpa' bo rnams ni phyag bzhi pa'o/_/rnal 'byor ma rnams ni phyag gnyis pa'o/_/rdo rje dang 
[\[26a.4\]]dril bu dang kha T+wAM ga dang cang te'u ni dpa' bo'i 'o/_/lha mo rnams kyis ni thod pa dang rdo rje dang sdigs mdzub po/_/thugs la sogs pa byin gyis rlob pa'i dus su ni rdo rje bzhi po 'ba' zhig gis so/_/sku gsung thugs kyi ting nge 'dzin sems dpa' bsgom pa snga ma bzhin du'o/_/
[\[26a.5\]]gsung rdo rje yang mdog dmar zhing dbang phyug chen po'i mgo bo bsnams nas pad+ma dang dril bu bsnams pa ni khyad par ro/_/dkar po dang dmar po dang sngon po dang ljang gu ni g.yon skor gyi phyogs kyi'o/_/ser po dang dkar po dang ljang gu dang nam mkha' ltar sngo bsangs ni g.yas 
[\[26a.6\]]skor gyi mtshams kyi'o/_/'khor lo'i shugs ma la sogs pa sku'i rdo rje ma yang snga ma bzhin te/_'khor lo dang dril bu dang tshangs pa'i mgo bo thogs pas tshangs pa'i pags pa bsnams pa ni khyad par ro/_/sangs rgyas mkha' 'gro ma la 'khyud pa ste/_de yang dkar mo 
[\[26a.7\]]thod pa dang 'khor lo thogs nas sdang ba rnams la sdigs mdzub byed pa'o/_/sngon po dang ljang gu dang dmar po dang ser po ni g.yon skor gyis ni mkha' 'gro ma la sogs pa'i 'o/_/nag po dang ser po dang dmar po dang ljang gu dang zla ba ltar dkar ba dang dud ka dang skya bo 
[\[26b\]]
[\[26b.1\]]dang dkar po ni phyogs dang mtshams kyi'o/_/oM shrI badzra he he ru ru kaM huM hUM phaT/_DA ki nI dzA la saM ba ra swA hA/_thugs kyi mkha' 'gro'i 'o/_/oM haM b+ha ga d+ha ka A ro li ka DA ki ni dzA la sam+ba ra hUM phaT swA hA/_pad+ma mkha' 'gro'i snying po'i 'o/_/oM A ro li ka dziM hUM phaT/_nye ba'i snying 
[\[26b.2\]]po'o/_/oM pad+ma DA ki nI hrIH hUM phaT swA hA/_oM sarba pad+ma DA ki nI ye AHhUM phaT swA hA/_lha mo'i snying po dang nye ba'i snying po'o/_/oM shrI DA ki nI dzA la sam+ba ra ru lu ru lu b+h+rUM hUM phaT swA hA/_oM b+h+rUM aM b+h+rUM hUM phaT/_sangs rgyas mkha' 'gro'i snying po dang nye ba'i snying po'o/_/oM bud+d+ha DA ki nI 
[\[26b.3\]]ye bai ro tsa nI ye b+hUM hUM phaT swA hA/_oM hUM sarba bud+d+ha DA ki nI ye hUM b+h+rUM phaT swA hA/_lha mo'i snying po dang nye ba'i snying po'o/_/rnal 'byor pa dman pa rnams kyi rab tu sbyor ba shes par bya ste/_sngags pas yungs kar gyi 'bras bu mang po la 'gegs pa'i ro'i khrag dang /_zhag 
[\[26b.4\]]gis yang dang yang byugs la tshes brgyad kyi skar ma rgyal la nyi ma tsha ba'i dus su gzhi la gzugs par bya ste/_gsang ba'i zhing sa zhag dang khrag gis bran pa dang de'i thod par bkug ste/_bskyed la smin pa'i sa bon ci rigs pa blangs te/_blo dang ldan pas mar khu'i 
[\[26b.5\]]rgyu me la ma reg par btsir nas mar khu sa la ma reg par bzhag ste sngags dang rnal 'byor gyis bsrung ngo /_/de nas yang 'gegs pa'i tshil las mar ngo'i nyin zhag khyad par can la/_mdun du maN+Dal byas la sngags pas cho ga bzhin du mchod nas dpal he ru ka dang 
[\[26b.6\]]gnyis su med pa [{]yi dgas [,]yi dwags [}]rnams la gtor ma sha dang khrag dang ldan pa byin la he ru ka'i spyod pas rnal 'byor chen po ngal so zhing sngags bzlas la byin gyis brlab par bya ste/_yi ge hUM gi snying po 'jigs pa'i sgra bsgrags par bya'o/_/de nas rlung gis me bskyod 
[\[26b.7\]]pa'i rlung gi dkyil 'khor gyi steng du rkang pa la hUM bkod pa'i rgyal mtshan bsams la sngar bstan pa'i mar khus byug par bya'o/_/de nas rkang mthil nas brtsams nas mgo bo'i bar du gu gul chen po'i bdug pas rgyun mi 'chad par bdugs la maN+Dal gyi me tog 
[\[27a\]]
[\[27a.1\]]gis rnal 'byor pa thams cad kyi bkas snying ga dang mgor brdegs la/_nga rgyal gyis sngags bzlas shing dril bu sgra drag tu dkrol nas skye gnas kyi phyag rgya bdag gi dpral bar bzhag pa bstan par bya'o/_/de'i tshe ro'i lus la bgegs sna tshogs 'byung 
[\[27a.2\]]ste bar chod byed do/_/de la sngags chen po grub pa'i rdzas kyis bgegs bzlog par bya ba'i phyir 'jigs par mi bya zhing drag po'i gzugs kyis dpa' bo'i dam tshig ci rigs par bstan par bya zhing /_bdud rtsi lnga dang chang du ldan pa'i thod par gtor ma byin la bdag la rnal 
[\[27a.3\]]'byor mkha' la spyod pa grub pa byin cig ces brjod par bya'o/_/de nas de'i lus la 'dzags pa'i mar khu blangs la ku ba la sogs pa gang yang rung bar blugs nas rkang mthil du byugs na nam mkha' la spyod pa'i dngos grub byed par 'gyur ro/_/yang sbur ter gyi 
[\[27a.4\]]sa bon la nya skyogs su byi dang ka'i khu ba dang khrag gis yang dang yang du sbags la gang yin pa'i thod par dur khrod kyi sas bkang bar bcug nas gtor ma dang mchod pa la sogs pa sngon bstan pa bzhin du ci rigs par byas la yongs su smin pa'i sa bon blangs la khar 
[\[27a.5\]]bcug na byar 'gyur ro/_/de la sogs pa'i sa bon khrag dang zhag gis yang dang yang du sbags shing sha dang rus phye'i bdug pas bdugs la de'i thod par bskyed de dus su smin pa'i 'bras bu blangs la khar bcug na de 'dra ba'i gzugs su mthong zhing tshe dang nad med pa 
[\[27a.6\]]phun sum tshogs par byed la de ltar khwa dang ce spyang dang khra dang rma bya dang khyim bya la sogs pa'i gzugs su byed do/_/kha nas bton na ni snga ma bzhin no/_/zla ba mar ngo'i skar ma rgyal dang /_gza' nyi ma la li ta la'i sa bon blangs te/_yang zla ba mar ngo'i gza' nyi ma 
[\[27a.7\]]la gcer bus ma du lung ga'i sa bon blangs la gang yang rung ba de'i yan lag gi mchog tu bcug la sha las bskyed par bya'o/_/rus pa'i snying po mar khu dang pags pa dang rus par bcas pas rig ma'i phyag brgyan te/_bdag nyid thal bas byugs nas de la blugs 
[\[27b\]]
[\[27b.1\]]par byas na 'bu srin la sogs pa'i nye bar 'tshe bas mi tshugs par 'gyur ro/_/'khrul 'khor gyi cho ga sngags dang ldan pas chu dron gyis mkhas pas rtag tu bkru bar bya'o/_/gshin rje mthar byed dang bgegs mthar byed sngags kyis mngon par bzlas pa'i bya ba dang 
[\[27b.2\]]bcas pas sha za la gtor ma sbyin no/_/gzhan du bton te khyer bar 'gyur ro/_/sangs rgyas spyan gyi sngags kyis ni gzugs la mA ma kI'i snying pos bkru bar bya'o/_/sgrol ma yis ni bskyed cing /_de bzhin du gos dkar mos ni bsrung bar bya'o/_/de ltar na skyon med 
[\[27b.3\]]cing bskyed na grub pa'i rdzas su gdon mi za'o/_/de bas ni de'i sa bon sngar bstan pa bzhin du blangs la lcags gsum gyis dkri bar bya'o/_/ma sha gsum dang ra ti phyed dang brgyad ni nyi ma'i tshad do/_/ma sha bzhi dang ra ti phyed dang gsum ni zla ba'i tshad do/_/ma sha gsum 
[\[27b.4\]]dang ra ti phyed dang lnga ni sreg byed kyi tshad do/_/de ltar [{]go rims [,]go rim [}]bzhin du nyi ma dang zla ba sreg byed rnams kyi ril bu dkris na blo dang ldan pas khar bcug na sbed par byed do/_/'jig rten gsum dang zla ba nyi mas mi mthong la kha nas bton na ni slar mthong bar 'gyur ro/_/yang lcags gsum 
[\[27b.5\]]gyis ma dkris par khar bcug na ni/_de ma thag tu ma du lung ga'i gzugs su 'gyur la bton na ni grol bar 'gyur ro/_/tshig 'phrog par 'gyur ro zhes bya ba la sogs pa ni sngags grub pa ste gzhan du ni ma yin no/_/de nas sngags kyi 'khor lo ni/_/cho ga las kyi mchog yin 
[\[27b.6\]]te/_nam mkha' dbyings kyi dbus su ni/_/sngags pas li khri'i mdog 'dra zhing /_/zla ba'i dkyil 'khor bsam par bya/_/de yi dbus dgod pad+ma ni/_/bcu drug 'dab mas brgyan pa ste/_/de dbus 'dab brgyad ge sar ldan/_/bcu drug 'dab ma'i shar phyogs la/_/
[\[27b.7\]]oM shI he he ru ru kaM badzra DA ki nI dzA la sam+ba ra/_dbus su ra dang hUM hUM phaT/_/phyogs rnams su ni g.yon skor ro/_/phyogs mtshams su ni thod pa ste/_/lte bar swA hA dang spel ba'i/_/bsgrub bya'i ming ni bri bar bya/_/dbus ni sna tshogs rdo rje phreng /_/phyi rol rdo rje'i phreng gis 
[\[28a\]]
[\[28a.1\]]bskor/_/'od dmar 'bar bas rnam brgyan par/_/[{]yi dgas [,]yi dwags [}]sen rtsi'i khu ba dang /_/tsan+dan dmar po bsres pa yis/_/yan lag 'od med kyis brgyan zhing /_/gcer bur gyur pas rang gis bri/_/ma mo'i khyim mnyam yid 'ong bar/_/shin tu 'jam po'i 
[\[28a.2\]]stan gnas shing /_/lha yi rnal 'byor dmigs nas su/_/skye gnas phyag rgya de nas bcing /_/bsgrub bya la ni mngon phyogs te/_/sngags bas brgya dang rtsa brgyad bzlas/_/tsan+dan dmar po'i rdul las ni/_/bsgrub par bya ba'i gzugs byas la/_/de yi lte bar 'khor lo gzhug_/
[\[28a.3\]]skye gnas nas ni skud pa dbyung /_/yang na sna yi bu ga nas/_/phyag rgya ldan pas mthe chung bskyod/_/skud pa bzung nas dus gsum du/_/yid la gnas pa'i 'khor lo bskyod/_/bram ze gsod kyang nyin bdun gyis/_/grub pa nyid du the tshom med/_/yang na tsha ba gsum byug 
[\[28a.4\]]la/_/spra tshil gyis kyang rab tu byug_/zangs ma'i khab kyis skye gnas dbug_/seng ldeng 'dab ma du med la/_/brgya rtsa brgyad du bzlas nas kyang /_/blo dang ldan pas nyin bdun gyis/_/bsgrub bya nges par 'gugs par 'gyur/_/de nas mi bdag bu mo sogs/_/'khor 
[\[28a.5\]]dang bcas pa'i ming bris la/_/bsgrub par bya ba'i rkang rjes la/_/tsan+dan dmar po nyid dang ni/_/dur khrod zas lhag thal ba rnams/_/sngags pas gcig tu byas nas ni/_/seng ldeng mdag me du med la/_/rdul gnyis lag gnyis kyis mnyes nas/_/sngags dang ldan pa'i 
[\[28a.6\]]sbyin sreg bya/_/de yi thal bas bsgrub bya yi/_/gzugs byas 'khor lo'i snying gar gzhug_/spra tshil gyis ni byugs la bsro/_/ci nas spra tshil ma zhu bar/_/nyi ma bdun du bsgrub byas na/_/lha yi 'jig rten 'ong 'gyur na/_/skye bo phal pa smos ci dgos/_/myos shing 
[\[28a.7\]]rmongs nas brgyal gyur nas/_/rlung gi shugs kyis 'ong bar 'gyur/_/'dis ni bcings par yang bya ste/_/thod pa skam po la ldong ros dang yung bas ming dang bsres pa'i 'khor lo bris la dur khrod dam lam gyi bzhi mdor sbas na/_ji srid 'tsho'i bar du bcings par 'gyur ro/_/
[\[28b\]]
[\[28b.1\]]dpal he ru ka zhes bya ba'i dka' 'grel sgrub pa'i thabs kyi gleng gzhi las le'u bcu pa'i rnam par bshad pa'o//_//de nas gzhan yang bshad par bya/_/zhes bya ba la sogs pas skye ba bdun pa la sogs pa'i phyugs bzung ba ste skye ba gzhan gyis bar ma chod pa'o/_/de'i 
[\[28b.2\]]mtshan nyid ni le'u bzhi bcu rtsa dgu pa las ston pas 'dir ma brjod pa'o/_/dpal he ru ka zhes bya ba'i dka' 'grel sgrub pa'i thabs kyi gleng gzhi las le'u bcu gcig pa'i rnam par bshad pa'o//_//de nas nye ba'i snying po bshad/_/ces bya ba ni/_oM hrIHha ha hUM hUM phaT te dgug 
[\[28b.3\]]pa la sogs pa'i don gyi las rnams bsdus pa'i snying po'o/_/'dab ma drug pa'i pad+ma yi/_/ge sar ze 'brus mtshan pa ste/_/zla bas nye bar mdzes byas shing /_/rdo rje phreng ba bzang pos brgyan/_/de yi phyi rol 'khor lo ni/_/zla ba'i 'od 'dra dri med bri/_/pad+ma drug la rig pa che/_/
[\[28b.4\]]yi ge g.yon nas bskor ba bri/_/bdud kyi las kun zlog byed pa'i/_/'khor lo'i rgyal po zhes bya ba'o/_/'khor lo de yi rtsibs la yang /_/rnal 'byor ma drug snying po ste/_/dbus su bdun pa he ru ka_/dur khrod sen rtsi ro tsa na/_/rang gi khu ba bcas pas bri/_/kha nas 
[\[28b.5\]]gsang sngags rgyal po zlos/_/rjes chags phyogs su lag brkyang ste/_/bsgrub bya mdun du bsams nas ni/_/rnal 'byor snyoms par 'jug pa yi/_/rjes su chags pas bsgrub byas na/_/lha yi 'jig rten 'ong 'gyur te/_/skye bo phal pa smos ci dgos/_/yang na 
[\[28b.6\]]tsan+dan dmar po dang /_/ske tshe lan tshwa de bzhin te/_/bsgrub bya'i rkang rjes sa dang ni/_/gcig tu byas nas btab par bya/_/de yi gzugs byas gro ga la/_/'khor lo bris nas snying gar gzhug_/gang gi ming smos bzlas nas su/_/bsgoms na nges par 'gugs par 
[\[28b.7\]]'gyur/_/sgrub pa'i sbyor ba 'di yis ni/_/sa yi bdag po nyid dang ni/_/nor dang skye bo dbang du 'gyur/_/skems shing zhes bya ba ni nus pa dang ldan pa'o/_/'di chu la yongs su bzlas la zhes bya ba ni lag pa la 'khor lo bsgoms pas so/_/mi'i khrag dang zhes bya ba ni dpa' 
[\[29a\]]
[\[29a.1\]]bo'i ro de nyid kyi'o/_/snod du zhes bya ba ni thod par ro/_/nyan mig ces bya ba la nyan zhes bya ba rna ba'o/_/de la reg pa tsam gyis mig rno bar 'gyur te/_'di ni gling mtha' nas rnyed pa'o/_/khrag dang zhes bya ba ni dpa' bo'i snying gi khrag dang ste/_yang dang yang du 
[\[29a.2\]]bsgos la grib ma la bskams pa'o/_/lcags gsum zhes bya ba sngar bstan pa bzhin no/_/lhag par gnas par byas la zhes bya ba ni byin gyis brlabs pa'i cho ga'i rim pa'o/_/dpal he ru ka'i zhal du bcug la zhes bya ba ni/_ro'i steng du dur khrod la sogs dben pa'i gnas 
[\[29a.3\]]su tshon sna lngas dkyil 'khor bris la/_de'i dbus su bsgrub par bya ba ste/_gu gul gyis bdug par bya'o zhes bya'o/_/nye ba'i snying po'i sngags 'dis zhes bya ba la sogs pa ni gsal lo/_/yang na nyi ma phyed na khro zhing nga rgyal gyis non pa'i sems kyis dur khrod kyi ras 
[\[29a.4\]]sam/_shing arka'i 'dab ma la mi'i rus pa'i smyu gus sngar bstan pa'i sol ba dang dug la sogs pa drag po'i rdzas kyis ji ltar bstan pa'i 'khor lo bris la/_dur khrod kyi thal bas bsgrub bya'i gzugs brnyan byas te snying gar bcug nas/_ske tshes byugs la yang dang 
[\[29a.5\]]yang du bsro bar byas na lus tsha bas btab ste sa la 'gyel bar 'gyur ro/_/de ro bsregs pa'i sa la sbas la mi rus kyi phur bus snying gar btab la tshigs thams cad tsher mas bkang ste/_snying gar chu tshan blugs na sdug bsngal chen po nyams su myong nas 'chi bar 
[\[29a.6\]]'gyur ro/_/yang na mi ro'i khar skyes pa'i yungs kar gyi 'bras bu blangs nas gang gi ming nas smos te gzugs brnyan la bsnun na de de'i mod la 'chi ba'am lus rims kyis 'debs par 'gyur zhing /_'o mas bkrus na slar yang thar bar 'gyur ro/_/dur khrod kyi ras la yung 
[\[29a.7\]]ba'am ldong ros kyis 'khor lo bris la tsan+dan dmar po'i rdul dang /_ske tshe dang lan tshwa dang de bzhin du bsgrub bya'i rkang rjes kyi rdul dang gcig tu byas pa'i phye ma lcags phye dang bsres la/_ras kyis phur la lam gyi bzhi mdor sor brgyad tsam brkos la dgra'i ming dang bcas 
[\[29b\]]
[\[29b.1\]]par lan bdun bzlas nas sbas na de ji srid 'tsho'i bar du bcings par 'gyur ro/_/bton nas 'o mas bkrus na slar rnal mar 'gyur ro/_/sme sha can gyi ro bsregs pa'i thal bas bsgrub bya'i gzugs byas nas gang yin pa'i ming dang 'khor lo bris nas snying gar bcug ste 
[\[29b.2\]]de nyid kyi thal bas lan bdun bzlas nas bsnun na de'i gzugs 'dzin par 'gyur ro zhes so/_/rims kyis thebs par bya ba la ni/[ {]yi dgas [,]yi dwags [}]kyi snying gar gang zhig gi ming dang spel nas 'khor lo bris pa de des thebs par 'gyur ro/_/lto na bar bya ba la ni [{]yi dgas [,]yi dwags [}]kyi 
[\[29b.3\]]klad pa la bya'o/_/brjed byed la ni rlung dang sprin gyi 'khor lo bya'o/_/gzer gdab pa la ni mdung rtse gsum pa la'o/_/dug dang mdze nad la ni lto 'phye la'o/_/gza' gang yin pa'i mtshan ma'i nang du chud par bris na des 'dzin par 'gyur ro/_/sbyin sreg gi me'i thal ba 
[\[29b.4\]]zhes bya ba ni sngar bstan pa'i sman gyi sbyin sreg gi thal bas gzugs byas la de'i snying gar 'khor lo bcug nas gang gi ming nas smos te bsnun na de lha nyid kyis kyang mi mthong ngo /_/chu la lan brgya rtsa brgyad bzlas nas gtor na slar yang snang bar 'gyur ro/_/thod pa kha 
[\[29b.5\]]sbyar gyi phyag rgya la zhes bya ba la khyi smyon pa'i thod pa kha sbyar gyi nang du gzhug par bya ste/_gang zhig gi ming dang spel ba'i 'khor lo smyo byed lnga dang ldan pa dbugs mi 'byin par lan bdun bzlas te bcug nas bcings la rgyas btab nas dur khrod du gang ming dang bcas 
[\[29b.6\]]nas sbas na de smyo bar 'gyur ro/_/de nyid kyis skye bo stong yang smyo bar 'gyur ro/_/bton nas 'o mas bkrus na slar yang grol bar 'gyur ro/_/sman bzang po bsgrubs nas khang stong du song ste lan brgya bzlas la bdag nyid kyi spyi bor bcings na zhes bya ba la sogs pa ste 
[\[29b.7\]]sgra ji bzhin par bstan te go sla'o/_/gzhan gyi dpung mngon du phyogs pa ni zhes bya ba la/_ldong ros sam yung bas lag pa g.yon pa'i mthil du 'khor lo 'di bris la lan stong du yongs su bzlas te khu tshur bcangs nas gzhan gyi dpung mngon par phyogs pa na yul du 
[\[30a\]]
[\[30a.1\]]'jug par bya'o/_/yang na zangs ma'i glegs mal ldong ros kyis sat+ta ka s+ya rak+Sha sarba a yud ni ba ra NaM zhes bya ba dang spel nas bris la cho ga bzhin mchod la spra tshil gyis g.yogs la lan stong du bzlas te lag pa g.yon par bzung la khu tshur bcangs 
[\[30a.2\]]la/_gsang sngags rgyal po brjod bya zhing /_/rgyan rnams kun gyis rnam brgyan nas/_/ji srid khu tshur ma grol bar/_/mtshon cha thams cad bcings par 'gyur/_/dgra bo gal te lag na ni/_/mtshon cha 'phyar bar byed pa yang /_/ji srid khu tshur ma grol bar/_/mtshon 
[\[30a.3\]]cha gtong bar mi nus so/_/de nas sman mchog bshad bya ste/_/mtshon cha lnga ni bzlog phyir ro/_/'khrul 'khor gyis 'phen mi 'phen dang /_/de bzhin lag pas 'phen pa dang /_/'phangs nas de ni 'dzin nyid dang /_/lag pas mtshon cha 'gyed dang lnga/_/'jig rten gsum po 
[\[30a.4\]]ma lus na/_/mtshon cha rnams ni 'di dag go/_/lag pas 'phen pa 'khor lo brjod/_/'khrul 'khor gyis 'phen mda' la sogs/_/'phangs nas gzung ba zhags sogs te/_/lag pa'i mtshon cha stobs 'gyed pa/_/mi 'phen ral gri sogs gnas pa/_/'khor lo sman ni 
[\[30a.5\]]legs mthong gis/_/mda' la sogs pa ni a kar na bi d+ha ka kas so/_/zhags pa la sogs pa'i dbyig pa ni ut+pa las so/_/lag pa'i mtshon cha ni gzhan gyis mi thub pa dkar pos so/_/ral gri la sogs pa ni smyo byed kyis so/_/sman bzang po de rnams nyi ma bzang 
[\[30a.6\]]po la blangs nas bdud rtsi lngas bsgos la sme sha can gyi bu mo sngon mo'i me tog gis byugs nas mar ngo'i tshes brgyad dam bcu bzhi la bram ze'i thod pa ro bsregs pa'i sas bkang bar bcug la/_ji skad bstan pa bzhin du chu blugs pa dang gtor ma sbyin pa la sogs pa 
[\[30a.7\]]byas nas smin pa'i lcug ma bzang po skar ma rgyal dang gza' nyi ma la blangs la mnyam par gzhag pas sa la ma lhung bar sngags kyi rgyal pos shin tu bsrungs la/_de dag thams cad lhan cig tu g.yon skor gyis bsgril la skud pa byas la mgrin pa'am mgo bor bcings 
[\[30b\]]
[\[30b.1\]]na mtshon cha slar bzlog cing mi tshugs par 'gyur la thams cad mtshon cha brgyas bsnun kyang gnod pa'i tshor ba mi 'byung zhing mtshon gyis mi chod la rdo rje'i lus su 'gyur ro/_/yang na dpa' chen gcig nyid kyis/_/mtshon cha thams cad zlog byed pa/_/pin ta ka ra zhes bya ba'i/_/
[\[30b.2\]]sman chen rnyed par gyur nas ni/_/sngags pas nyi ma bzang po la/_/de yi 'bras bu yongs smin pa/_/sngon bstan rim pa'i sbyor ba yis/_/bram ze'i thod par sa bon gzugs/_/de nas shing smin pa dang gza' nyi ma la nyi ma shar ba'i dus su de'i rtsa ba phra mo rnams kha byang du phyogs 
[\[30b.3\]]pas blangs nas sa la ma lhung bar bzung ste ji ltar bstan pa'i rim pas phrag pa dang spyi bor bzung na/_rgyu ba dang mi rgyu ba thams cad rmongs par 'gyur ro/_/yang de'i ge sar zho dang lhan cig bza' ste/_ji srid mgrin par lud kyi bar du dpa' bos bza'o/_/de ji srid ma zhu ba de 
[\[30b.4\]]srid du rdo rje 'dzin pa dang 'dra ba'i lus su 'gyur te/_de la mtshon chas phog kyang chu'i thigs pa ltar gnod par mi 'gyur ro/_/yang na sngon gyi go cha'i rim pa'i sngags pas bram ze'i thod par bcugs pa'i a shwat+tha'i rtsa ba blangs la cho ga bzhin du ril bu bya'o/_/nyi ma zla ba 
[\[30b.5\]]sreg byed pa'i/_/so so'i cha la gnas pa yi/_/lcags gsum gyis ni dkris byas pa'i/_/ril bu kha ru gzhug par bya/_/de nas dpa' bo chen pos mtshon cha thams cad bzlog cing rims dang /_lto na ba dang /_dug dang /_mkha' 'gro'i gnod pa dang /_gza' rnams de'i 
[\[30b.6\]]dri tshor ba tsam gyis rab tu 'bros te/_seng ge'i dris glang po che 'bros pa bzhin no/_/yang pe ta'i sa bon bram ze'i klad pa la cho ga bzhin du btsugs la sman bzang po blang bar bya ste/_skar ma rgyal dang gza' nyi ma dang zla ba la mchod pa dang gtor ma byas na phyag rgya dang bcas pa'i 
[\[30b.7\]]sgrub pa po lha'i rnal 'byor gyis sngags zlos pas snga ma bzhin gcer bu skra grol bar gyur pa mnyam par gzhag pas mtha' dag btus te/_rnal 'byor ma'i lag par thod pa sa la ma lhung bar gzhag go/_/de nas dum bu bdun byas la skud pa dkar pos dkris nas gang gi 
[\[31a\]]
[\[31a.1\]]spyi bor bzung na sa stengs na ji snyed pa'i mtshon cha du ma rnams brlag par 'gyur te/_dper na nyi mas mun pa bzhin no/_/de nas 'dam gyi cho ga bshad/_/nag po chen po sman chen po/_/rgyal dang nyi ma la btsal nas/_/mchod dang gtor ma yang dag ldan/_/
[\[31a.2\]]dgongs mo'i dus su snyan mnan la/_/tho rangs lha yi bdag nyid dang /_/gcer bu skra grol gyur pa yis/_/shin tu mnyam par gzhag bas blang /_/khyu mchog rwa yis blangs pa yis/_/phyi nas lhan cig bsre bar bya/_/gzhon nu ma yis btags nas su/_/de 
[\[31a.3\]]yis lus ni bsku bar bya/_/ji srid lus la gnas kyi bar/_/de srid mtshon chas chod mi 'gyur/_/dug kyang lus la mi 'phog ste/_/bdag nyid chen pos sman bza'o/_/yang ku mA ri blangs la gzhon nu ma'i rkang rjes kyi sa dang ldan par byas la lug gi rwa dang cha 
[\[31a.4\]]mnyam par byas te btags pa'i 'dam bskus na sum cu pas kyang mi chod do/_/yang tsa na li'i sdong bu blangs la mtshan gcig pa dang shing gcig pa'i sa dang ldan par byas pa'i sbyor bas byugs pa'i lus ni sum cu pas kyang mi shigs so/_/de dag gi lo gcig gi nus 
[\[31a.5\]]pa can de phyi nas ni gzhan bya'o/_/de nas sba bya dpa' chen pos/_/dpa' bo thams cad mchod byas te/_/mngal nas dang po phyung ba ni/_/ma yi nu zho ma 'thungs par/_/shi ba'i ro ni rnyed nas ni/_/he ru ka dpal gnyis med mchod/_/gtor ma mnyam par gzhag pas 
[\[31a.6\]]bsrung /_/ltag pa nas ni gshags nas su/_/de yi lce ni blang bar bya/_/b+h+ring+ga ra dza du du ra/_/kaM kaM dza ga gzhan mi thub/_/rgyal dang nyi ma la blangs nas/_/lnga po rnams dang ldan pa ni/_/btags te ril bu byas nas su/_/grib ma la ni bskams nas kyang /_/lcags 
[\[31a.7\]]gsum gyis ni dkri bar bya/_/ra ti phyed brgyad ma sha gsum/_/ra ti phyed gsum ma sha bzhi/_/ra ti lnga dang ma sha gsum/_/rim pa bzhin du dkri bar bya/_/dkris nas yang dag mchod byas la/_/stong du bzlas te khar bcug na/_/ji srid kha nas gnas pa ru/_/sa gnas 
[\[31b\]]
[\[31b.1\]]mtshon cha du ma yang /_/bltas pa tsam gyis 'jig 'gyur te/_/dper na 'bar mes rtswa bzhin no/_/rims dang lto nad dug dang ni/_/mkha' 'gro ma yi gnod pa dang /_/gza' rnams thams cad zlog byed pa/_/rdo rje sems dpa'i bka' bzhin no/_/de nas gzhan yang bshad 
[\[31b.2\]]bya ba/_/sems can thams cad rab rmongs pa/_/rgyal gyi nyin par nan tan du/_/nags kyi ras bal blangs nas su/_/bu mo gzhon nu'i skud pas bya/_/g.yon phyogs su ni bkal nas su/_/sum sgril du ni skud pa bya/_/rtsa ba'i sngags ni stong phrag brgyad/_/mngon par 
[\[31b.3\]]bzlas shing btags nas ni/_/mdud pa byas nas lag g.yon gyis/_/de ru ma mo rnams ni brtag_/nyi shu rtsa bzhi gnyis med pa/_/'gog pa'i ting 'dzin la gnas te/_/yang dag mchod pa byas nas su/_/lag pa g.yas pa bcing bar bya/_/mtshon cha thams 
[\[31b.4\]]cad zlog byed pa/_/ral gri mda' sogs gzhan du min/_/nags skyes shal ma li yi bal/_/blo dang ldan pas rgyal la blang /_/gzhon nu ma yi lag pa ni/_/sa yis phyi nas bkal bar bya/_/dge ba'i rgyu skar skar ma dang /_/zla ba stobs can dag la ni/_/sngon du 
[\[31b.5\]]gsungs pa'i bya ba yis/_/shin tu mnyam gzhag mdud pa bya/_/'di ni yid la gzung byas pas/_/sum cu pas kyang mi 'ching zhing /_/mtshon cha thams cad 'ching 'gyur pas/_/las kyi cho ga blta bar bya/_/de ltar gsang ba'i lta ba las/_/gsang chen gsang 
[\[31b.6\]]bas sba bya ste/_/blo dang ldan pas thams cad du/_/skal med sems can la mi sbyin/_/dpal he ru ka zhes bya ba'i dka' 'grel sgrub pa'i thabs kyi gleng gzhi las le'u bcu gnyis pa'i rnam par bshad pa'o//_//de nas zhes bya ba ni de ma thag tu'o/_/cho ga'i las rnams 
[\[31b.7\]]ni/[ {]go rims [,]go rim [}]bzhin du bshad bya ba/_zhes bya ba ni/_oM ha na maHhi swA hA hu bau ShaT he hUM hUM ho phaT haM ste re re zhing ngam sbyor ba'i tshig gi yi ge drug gi gzugs dang dpa' bo'i lha ci rigs par sbyar ro/_/de la cho ga zlos bzhin du/_ya nga ba'i lam du zhugs na zhes bya ba la 
[\[32a\]]
[\[32a.1\]]sogs pa ni zlos bzhin pas yid la ci 'dod pa thams cad byed do zhes pa'o/_/de nas dbus su dpa' bo'i sngags/_/de nyid rig pa'i bsam gtan gyis/_/dpa' bo'i 'khor lo rab sbyor bas/_/de yis bya ba kun bya'o/_/de la rim pa 'dis bri bar bya ste/_
[\[32a.2\]]zla ba'i dbus gyur rdo rje ni/_/rtse gsum de bzhin kha drug pa/_/de yi dbus gyur pad+ma ni/_/'dab ma drug la legs gnas pa/_/sngags pas ze'u 'brur bri bar bya/_/ji ltar bstan bzhin bsgrub bya'i ming /_/rdo rje sems sogs sangs rgyas kyis/_/ji ltar go cha'i 
[\[32a.3\]]sngags bshad bzhin/_/sngon sngags dbus su bri bya ste/_/gzhan ni pad 'dab la de bzhin/_/ring po drug ni yongs spangs par/_/rdo rje'i lte bar dgod par bya/_/gur gum sen rtsi rang khrag dang /_/ro tsa na yis gro ga la/_/rgyal dang nyi dus sgrub pa pos/_/cho ga 
[\[32a.4\]]bzhin du bri bar bya/_/dus gsum du ni bzlas byas nas/_/mchod chen byas nas gzung bya ste/_/sngags pas lan bdun sngags btab nas/_/gos kyi tha mar bcing bar bya/_/lam du zhugs na rkun po dang /_snag gis 'jigs par mi 'gyur 
[\[32a.5\]]ro/_/thams cad du ni bsrung 'gyur te/_/grub pa nyid du the tshom med/_/yang 'khor lo de nyid kyi lte ba la thig le lta bu'i rgyan byas pa'i thig les bskor nas/_bsgrub bya 'khor dang bcas pa mo ha na zhes bya ba'i tshig dang spel la gos kyi tha ma la mdud de/_chos kyi 
[\[32a.6\]]dbyings su bsgoms nas zlos bzhin du 'gro na lha dang mi rnams kyis mthong bar mi 'gyur ro/_/yang bi sha da ta ka ri'i sa bon bram ze'i thod par cho ga bzhin btsugs la bskyed pa dang /_zla ba gzas zin pa'i dus su byang phyogs su zug pa'i rtsa ba blangs la lcags 
[\[32a.7\]]gsum gyis dkris te lan stong bzlas na go char 'gyur ro/_/yang na n+ya gro d+ha'i 'bras bu khwa nag pos zos pa'i rtug pa'i 'bras bu blangs nas nyi ma bzang po la/_bram ze'i thod par ro bsregs pa'i sa yis bkang bar btsugs nas bdud rtsi lngas gtor te skyes 
[\[32b\]]
[\[32b.1\]]nas 'dab ma gnyis tsam blangs la lcags gsum gyis dkris la mchod pa rnams kyis mchod nas bsngags te khar bcug na/_lha dang mi rnams kyis mi mthong bar 'gyur ro/_/go cha bzlas pa'i lag pas gang la reg pa de zhes bya ba la dur khrod kyi 
[\[32b.2\]]ras la ba ra tsi ta ka'i khu ba blangs la ldong ros dang yung ba'i a shwa ka'i smyu gus 'khor lo thig les mtshan pa bris te phyi nas bsgrub bya dpung dang bzhon par bcas pa'i ming dang spel nas lan stong bzlas nas/_yang bsgrub bya'i rkang rjes kyi rdul dang dur khrod kyi thal 
[\[32b.3\]]ba la bsgrub bya'i gzugs byas te/_'khor lo'i snying gar bcug nas me tog ser pos gtor zhing rkang pa g.yon pas mnan la brgyad brgya bzlas nas sbas te/_de'i steng du snying gar gnas pa'i khang pa byas la gang gi ming gzung ba de mdung bzhin du rengs 
[\[32b.4\]]par 'gyur te/_sum cu rtsa gsum pas kyang bskyod par mi nus so/_/gzhan gyi dmag tshogs sgrol bar bya ba la ni 'o mas bkus na slar rnal mar 'gyur ro/_/rang gi khrag la zhes bya ba la sogs pa gsal lo/_/khro bo'i rgyal po'i rnam par bsgoms la 'di ltar chu'i 
[\[32b.5\]]rdul phra rab thams cad dkyil 'khor gyi lhar bskyed pa'i dbu rgyan gyi gzugs kyis ni chu'i steng du 'gro bar 'gyur ro/_/rnal 'byor ma dang dpa' bo'i 'khor lo snyoms par zhugs pa ni lag pa g.yon pa dang g.yas pa gnyis kyis mnyes na rigs drug gi rnal 'byor 
[\[32b.6\]]ma dbang du 'gyur ro/_/skar ma rgyal la ras byas la zhes bya ba la sbrul gyi rgyal po rnyed nas dkar po dang nag po'i ri mo thams cad so sor dum bu dum bur bcang la nag po dang dkar po'i dbye bas so sor bya ste/_ras bal gyi sa bon blangs nas dkar po'i zla ba la rgyal 
[\[32b.7\]]gyi nyin par dum bu dkar po zhag dang shas bsgos la sa la sbas te gdab po/_/ji ltar nag po'i dum bu'i zhag dang shas kyang sa bon bsgos shing /_zla ba mar ngo'i rgyal gyi nyin par de nyid kyi zhag dang shas sa la yang sbag go/_/dkar po dang nag po'i cha'i bye 
[\[33a\]]
[\[33a.1\]]brag gis ji ltar bskyed pa'i ras bal blangs la rgyal gyi nyi ma gzhan la dkar po'i chas bskyed pa'i ras bal la/_rnal 'byor ma ser skyas bkal ba de'i nyin bar thag pa ser skyas btag go/_/de ltar nag po'i chas bskyod pa'i ras bal tshes nag po'i 
[\[33a.2\]]skar mo rgyal la rnal 'byor ma sngon mos bkal zhing de nyid kyi nyi ma la thag pa sngon pos ji ltar lus yog par gyur pa de ltar btags par byas la sngon po dang dkar po'i cha yis rgyal gyi nyi ma gzhan la bsgrub par bya ste/_ras la stong phrag sum cu rtsa dgu mngon par bsngags la brgyad 
[\[33a.3\]]brgya bzlas shing bdug par bya ste/_a ka ru nag po dang dum bu nag po'i sha dang rus pa zla ba mar ngo la bskyed pa'i ras la bdug go/_/de ltar zla ba mar ngo la bskyed pa'i ras ni dum bu dkar po'i sha dang rus pa dang sang ka puSh+pa dang bcas pas bdug par bya'o/_/de nas 
[\[33a.4\]]gus pas bla ma dang dpa' bo dang rnal 'byor ma rnams mchod la/_lha'i rnal 'byor dmigs te/_rgyal la bsgrub pa'i ras la rnal 'byor ma'i bka' lung gis bsgo bar bya'o/_/de gyon pa'i lus kyis stong phrag bcu bzlas nas 'grub par 'gyur te/_grib ma mi mngon zhing
