[\[37b.3\]]pa la dga' dpung pa 'phyang /_/yan lag rtsub cing skra ring ba/_/so sna sen mo 'jam pa ste/_/rkang pa mnyam zhing sna ring la/_/lto ni bum pa ltar sbom zhing /_/bum pa'i 'phyang ba la mdzes pa'i bzhin/_/zhi dang rgyas la'ang sbyar bar bya/_/phur bu ma ni mig g.yo zhing /_/'og mig 
[\[37b.4\]]can la gdong srab pa/_/so snang lus kyi sha srab cing /_/skra chu 'dod drag na 'bab pa/_mgo skya bus pa ltar 'gro zhing /_/sgur zhing lus bong chung ba ste/_/lag pa thung zhing nu ma mtho/_/smin sbom dpral chung byin yang sbom/_/de yis reg na mi 'tsho ste/_/nye du rnams ni brlag 
[\[37b.5\]]par byed/_/nag po'i las la sbyar bar bya/_/brgyad pa [{]ri dgas [,]ri dwags [}]ma skal bzang /_/lto ba chung zhing rkang pa chung /_/[{]ri dgas [,]ri dwags [}]mgrin pa [{]ri dgas [,]ri dwags [}]mig_/skra ni phra zhing snum pa ste/_/so yang sral mchu snum pa/_/gzugs mdzes thig le lta bu'i byang /_/reg 'jam sred pa mang 
[\[37b.6\]]du smra/_/sgra ni bya rog skad lta bu/_/rtag tu gcer bu dbul ba yin/_/sems rtsub pa la sdig la dga'/_/skur 'debs ma de [{]yi dgas [,]yi dwags [}]rigs/_/dbang gi las la sbyar bar bya/_/'di brgyad du ni rab tu grags/_/thod pa dang dgra sta la sogs pa'i mtshan ma brgyad ni/_ji ltar 
[\[37b.7\]]bstan pa'i rigs kyi [{]go rims [,]go rim [}]bzhin du gzugs can ma la sogs pa'i phyag dang po'i mtshan ma'o/_/gzhan gyi kha T+wAM ga dang thod pa nas khrag dang khu ba gsol ba'o/_/dpal he ru ka zhes bya ba'i dka' 'grel sgrub pa'i thabs kyi gleng gzhi las le'u bcu bdun pa'i rnam par bshad 
[\[38a\]]
[\[38a.1\]]pa'o//_//de nas yang dag bshad bya ba/_/mkha' 'gro ma yi go 'phang mchog_/ces bya ba la mchog ni mang po'i rigs te/_mang por 'gyur ba'i rigs rnams so/_/gang yang gsungs pa/_dam pa'i rigs ni rnam pa brgya/_/zhes pa'o/_/rab tu 
[\[38a.2\]]gnas pas na go 'phang ste/_/gang gi mtshan nyid gsung ba de nyid kyis yongs su dag pa'o/_/dam tshig ces bya ba ni rnal 'byor pa rnams kyi rjes su 'jug pa ste/_des gnas pa'o/_/dpal he ru ka zhes bya ba'i dka' 'grel sgrub pa'i thabs kyi gleng gzhi las le'u bco 
[\[38a.3\]]brgyad pa'i rnam par bshad pa'o//_//de nas yang dag bshad bya ba/_/zhes bya ba ni mdog dang mtshan ma'i rjes su'o/_/lA ma zhes bya ba ni yid du 'ong ba reg pa la sogs pa'i yon tan gyi mtshan nyid dang ldan pa ste/_yan lag thams cad yongs su rdzogs pa'o/_/
[\[38a.4\]]gang gi bzhin zhes bya ba la sogs pa ni go sla'o/_/srin mo zhes bya ba ni sbyor ba gcig pa'o/_/pad+ma'i phyag rgya zhes bya ba ni/_lag pa gnyis pad+ma skor bar sbyin par bya'o/_/yang na pad+ma'i phyag rgya ni pad+ma'i phyag rgya ste/_gang du ji lta bar/_langs pa'am ni stan dag la/_/mdun 
[\[38a.5\]]du skal bzang bzhag nas ni/_/pad+ma rab tu rgyas par bya/_/myur du gnyis gnyis sbyor ba yis/_/mkhas pas rdo rjes rgyas gdab bya/_/rus sbal gyi phyag rgya ni lag pa gnyis thal mo sbyar ba'o/_/rus sbal phyag rgya b+ha gar ni/_/thal mo brdeg dang ldan reg bya/_/phyag rgya sngags 
[\[38a.6\]]kyi sbyor ba yis/_/sa yis ma mchu btsir bar byed/_/mi thub [{]ri dgas [,]ri dwags [}]lpags pa'o/_/yang na 'jam zhing mtshan nyid bzang /_/mi thub stan du shes par bya/_/spyi blugs thod pa zhes brjod de/_/yang na spyi blugs lus su brjod/_/lag gnyis mgrin par dam 
[\[38a.7\]]'khyud nas/_/ji ltar skyil krung bcas nas 'khyud/_/ji ltar phyag rgya rtogs 'gyur ba/_/phyag rgya'i lan du brjod pa yin/_/tshes bcu bzhi zhes bya ba la sogs pa go sla'o/_/yang na tshes bcu bzhi'i gnas skabs ji ltar rnyed/_pad+ma rnam par rgyas shing 'bar dang ldan/_/
[\[38b\]]
[\[38b.1\]]dpal ldan gang gi sems ni gang mnyam pa/_/de ni pad+ma can gyi mtshan nyid yin/_/mchu gnyis glo zhing zhes bya ba la sogs pa ni go sla'o/_/mdung zhes bya ba ni ser mo gcig bstan pa'i phyag rgya sbyin par bya'o/_/de nas rdo rje skyil krung gis/_/rang gi rdo rje der gnas pa'o/_/
[\[38b.2\]]gar nyid kyang ni bstan par bya/_/zhes bya ba ni/_dam du 'khyud nas 'o byed cing /_/byed pas bsngams pa'i phyag rgya rnams/_/rab tu bskor ba byas nas ni/_/glang chen ma dang bde sgrub ma'o/_/bcu bzhi zhes bya ba ni go sla'o/_/ci nus pas mchod par bya'o zhes bya ba'o/_/dpal 
[\[38b.3\]]he ru ka zhes bya ba'i dka' 'grel sgrub pa'i thabs kyi gleng gzhi las le'u bcu dgu pa'i rnam par bshad pa'o//_//de nas gzhan yang bshad bya ba/_/lag pa g.yon gyi brda rnams te/_/zhes bya ba la brda rnams kyi gnyis ka'i rang bzhin gcig tu gyur nas ming po 
[\[38b.4\]]dang sring mo ltar shin tu gdung ba'i dbang du gyur nas bsgom pa thams cad byed do zhes bya ba'o/_/dpal he ru ka zhes bya ba'i dka' 'grel sgrub pa'i thabs kyi gleng gzhi las le'u nyi shu pa'i rnam par bshad pa'o//_//de nas gsang ba chen po ni/_/mtshan nyid nga 
[\[38b.5\]]yis bshad par bya/_/zhes bya ba la nga yis zhes bya ba ni rdo rje 'chang bas te/_snying rje chen po'i dbang gis rnal 'byor pa rnams kyi thabs gang gis sgrub po 'grub par 'gyur ba de nyid ni gsang ba chen po'i mtshan nyid de/_/nyan thos la sogs pa'i spyod yul ma yin pa'i phyir 
[\[38b.6\]]ro/_/bud gang spyi gtsug reg byed pa/_/zhes bya ba la sogs pa ni sgra ji bzhin pa ste go sla'o/_/dpal he ru ka zhes bya ba'i dka' 'grel sgrub pa'i thabs kyi gleng gzhi las le'u nyi shu rtsa gcig pa'i rnam par bshad pa'o//_//de nas gzhan yang rab bshad pa/_/
[\[38b.7\]]yan lag phyag rgya'i khyad par ni/_/zhes bya ba ni 'dir gsang ba chen po'i mtshan nyid do/_/gzhan zhes bya ba ni dam pa ste/_gang gis rnal 'byor ma rnams mnyes par 'gyur ba'o/_/khyad par zhes bya ba la lag pa'i spyi las khyad par ni sor mo rnams so/_/gang gis shes 
[\[39a\]]
[\[39a.1\]]pas zhes bya ba la sogs pa ni sgra ji bzhin pa'o/_/dpal he ru ka zhes bya ba'i dka' 'grel sgrub pa'i thabs kyi gleng gzhi las le'u nyi shu rtsa gnyis pa'i rnam par bshad pa'o//_//de nas zhes bya ba la sogs pa ni/_sgra ji bzhin pa ste go sla'o/_/gzhir ni 
[\[39a.2\]]sa gzhi byas nas su/_/zhes bya ba ni stan las ldang ba'o/_/mchod ces bya ba ni dam por 'khyud dang 'o bya ba'o/_/smin ma g.yo zhes bya ba ni/_cung zad ngo tsha ba'i phyir ro/_/rnal 'gyur bskor ba zhes bya ba ni/_phul du byung ba'i bde ba sgrub pa'i don gyis bskor ba ni byang chub kyi sems 
[\[39a.3\]]la yongs su bskor ba ste/_srog dang rtsol ba sdom pa'i 'khrul 'khor gyis yang dag par phul du byung ba'i bde ba chen po'i lte ba'i dkyil 'khor du 'bar bas stong pa dbus su chud pa'i sngags pas 'jig rten gsum po dang lhan cig tu 'dre ba ste/_sa dang chu dang rlung nyid dang /_/
[\[39a.4\]]me dang nam mkha' nyid dang ni/_/bdag dang gzhan du rtag tshor dang /_/skad cig de la kun mi dmigs/_/de'i mod la ldog 'gyur te/_/bdag gis bde ba nyams su myong /_/zhes so/_/khyim du zhes bya ba la sogs pa ni go sla'o/_/yang na/_b+ha ga'i dbus chung rdo rje ni/_/shin tu 
[\[39a.5\]]stong par bsgoms pa ni/_/thams cad nam mkha' dang mnyam pa'o/_/ma rig la sogs glo bur ba/_/rtog pa'i nyes pa rtog las byung /_/rang bzhin 'od gsal rnam dag pa/_/sems ni me long bzhin du blta/_/chos rnams gzugs brnyan lta bu ste/_/gsal zhing 
[\[39a.6\]]dag pa nam mkha' mtshungs/_/ral gri nyon mongs gcod byed pa/_/sems can don byed rno ba'o/_/bsod nams kyis 'degs rgyal mtshan bzhin/_/mdung thung nyon mongs 'bigs byed pas/_/sems ni rtse gcig pa nyid do/_/de nyid me rtse rnal 'byor pas/_/kha 
[\[39a.7\]]sbyar bde ba thob pa'o/_/mtshan ma'i cha byad gsal zhes bya/_/zhes bya ba ni rdo rje dang 'khor lo dang rin po che dang pad+ma dang ral gri dang sna tshogs rdo rje rnams ni rgyal mtshan dang /_mdung thung rnams ji lta ba ste/_de bzhin gshegs pa rnams kyi rigs kyi dbye bas rdo rje dang rgyal mtshan dang 
[\[39b\]]
[\[39b.1\]]ral gri dang mdung thung ni nas kyi 'bru tshad tsam mo/_/'khor lo dang rin po che dang pad+ma dang sna tshogs rdo rje rnams ni sran tsa na ka'i tshad tsam ste/_mi bskyod pa la sogs pa'i rim pas ji lta ba bzhin rang rang gi mtshan ma kha dog lnga po'i nang du dkyil 'khor gyi 'khor lo chud pa la/_sems brtan 
[\[39b.2\]]pa phra mo'i rnal 'byor sgom pa las/_rnal 'byor pas phyag rgya'i dngos grub yid kyi rang bzhin bzang po 'grub par 'gyur ro/_/dpal he ru ka zhes bya ba'i dka' 'grel sgrub pa'i thabs kyi gleng gzhi las le'u nyi shu rtsa gsum pa'i rnam par bshad pa'o//_//de nas gzhan 
[\[39b.3\]]yang brda yi skad/_/ces bya ba la yul sna tshogs kyi khyad par gyi skad ni brda zhes bya ba kla klo ste/_rnal 'byor ma rnams kyi dam tshig gi phyag rgya'o/_/pro taM gi zhes bya ba ni gsang por smra ba zhes bya ba la sogs pa ni/_sgra ji bzhin pa nyid kyis na go sla ba ste/_gang zhig brda yi skad rnams 
[\[39b.4\]]shes pa ni/_/'dir rnal 'byor pa nyid du the tshom med do/_/dpal he ru ka zhes bya ba'i dka' 'grel sgrub pa'i thabs kyi gleng gzhi las le'u nyi shu rtsa bzhi pa'i rnam par bshad pa'o//_//de nas don kun 'dod pa yi/_/sba bya brgyad mtha' sbyar byas te/_/zhes bya ba la brgyad kyi 
[\[39b.5\]]mtha' ni yi ge ha ste/_yi ge u dang thig ler bcas pa'o/_/oM na mo b+ha ga wa te bI re shA ya/_zhes bya ba la sogs pa ni go sla'o/_/dpal he ru ka zhes bya ba'i dka' 'grel sgrub pa'i thabs kyi gleng gzhi las le'u nyi shu rtsa lnga pa'i rnam par bshad pa'o//_//de nas gzhan 
[\[39b.6\]]yang zhes bya ba ni dpal he ru ka'i bdag nyid chen po'o/_/lugs 'byung lugs las bzlog pa yis/_/zhes bya ba ni rnal 'byor gyi bslab pa'i rim pa las ma 'das pa'o/_/'og dang steng gi dngos grub ste/_/zhes bya ba la 'og ni sa 'og la sogs pa gang ci yod pa'o/_/
[\[39b.7\]]steng gis zhes bya ba ni steng gi dngos grub ste phyag rgya chen po'o/_/pho nya dang 'dra bar gyur pas na pho nya ste byang chub pa'i phun sum tshogs pa thams cad du nges shing thams cad du gnyis su med pa'i dga' ba'i rang bzhin gyis legs par gnas pa'i pho nya'o/_/bdag gi pho 
[\[40a\]]
[\[40a.1\]]nya ma zhes bya ba ni spyod pa'i mchog lhan cig tu spyod pa'o/_/kun 'gro zhes bya ba ni skal ba ldan pa'i sems can rnams kyi don bsgrub par bya ba'i phyir 'o bya ba dang rab tu 'khyud pa la sogs pa thams cad ster bas so/_/rnal 'byor gnas kyi khyad par nyid/_/
[\[40a.2\]]ces bya ba la/_gnas ni sa ru rab tu bshad/_/pad+ma'i dkyil 'khor gnas su gsungs/_/gnyis med dga' ba'i rang bzhin rgyal/_/bde chen gang du gnas pas na/_/de phyir mchog gi pad+ma gnas/_/khu khrag 'dres shing gang ba ni/_/khyad par du ni 'o byas nas/_/lce yis yang 
[\[40a.3\]]dag dril nas blangs/_/chang 'thung rab tu dga' ba yis/_/rdo rje pad+ma mnyam dgar sbyar/_/ji snyed rnal 'byor tshogs rnams ni/_/zhes bya ba la dga' ba'i bstan bcos las bshad pa'i byed lugs de snyed rnams rdo rje sems dpas gsungs te/_byang chub tu grub pas tha mal 
[\[40a.4\]]pa'i mtshan nyid kyi dbye ba shes par bya'o/_/de nyid kyis na/_bden can kun la sbyin bya yi/_/gzhan du nam yang ma yin no/_/zhes gsungs so/_/ma dang sring mo zhes bya ba la sogs pa rnams ni sgra ji bzhin pa'o/_/gang zhig rtag tu sngags sbyin pa/_/zhes bya ba ni phyag rgya de nyid 
[\[40a.5\]]dang sbyor bas shes rab ye shes so zhes bya'o/_/rnyed dka'i dam tshig nga yis bshad/_/ces bya ba la sogs pa ni go sla'o/_/he ru ka las sbyor gzhan med/_/ces bya ba ni gang zhig bstan pa de thams cad he ru ka'i sbyor bas bya'i gzhan gyis ni ma yin no zhes bya ba'i don no/_/
[\[40a.6\]]de nyid kyis na/_slob dpon rnal 'byor ma nyid kyi/_/zhes pa ste/_rnal 'byor pa rnams kyi ma ni rnal 'byor ma'o/_/de'i phyir brnyas mi bya zhes bya ba la sogs pa gsungs te/_rnal 'byor gzhan dang kun sbyor pas/_/zhes bya ba la/_snying rje chen por rab dga' ba'i/_/rnal 'byor 
[\[40a.7\]]mchog gi thabs spangs nas/_/'dod lha'i dngos grub bsgrub don du/_/rnal 'byor gzhan la brten byas pas/_/gang zhig dngos grub 'dod gyur pa/_/dmus long bzhin du rnam par rmongs/_/rgyal srid las nyams rgyal po bzhin/_/nges par sdug bsngal gyis rmongs 
[\[40b\]]
[\[40b.1\]]'gyur/_/dam tshig spyod pa'i spyod pa bya/_/zhes bya ba la sogs pa sna tshogs pa gsungs pa ste/_rnam par mi rtog sems kyis ni/_/rigs rnams thams cad mnyam par sbyar/_/dbang po gnyis ni mnyam sbyor bas/_/nyan pa la sogs dbang po kun/_/bsre zhing bde bar 
[\[40b.2\]]bsdu bar bya/_/gtso bo gang du spyod pa yi/_/tshangs spyod stong pa nyid du 'gyur/_/brjed med dran pa'i spyod pa yis/_/bde ba rtogs par byed pa'i gzhi/_/rdzogs pa'i byang chub lam dag pa'i/_/de nyid bla na med pa ni/_/dam tshig spyod pa rab tu gsungs/_/byang chub 
[\[40b.3\]]bsgrub la gnas pa yi/_/sgrub pa po yis rtag zhes bya/_/rgyud kun gyi ni thun mong du/_/zhes bya ba ni bya ba dang spyod pa dang rnal 'byor dang rnal 'byor ma rnams dang /_rnal 'byor bla ma'i rgyud chen po'i rang bzhin du bya ba'i sbyor ba dang mtshungs pa ma yin no/_/mi gzhom 
[\[40b.4\]]zhes bya ba ni/_mnyam pa'i bdag nyid du blta bar mi bya ste/_rgyud de dang de rnams ni rgyu ma yin pa'i phyir ro/_/dam tshig mi spyod shin tu sbas/_/gang la mi sbyin mi bya ste/_/skal ldan sems can rnams la bshad/_/kha la sogs pa brgya po 'di/_/mos dang dad pas 
[\[40b.5\]]'phel byed pas/_/zhes bya ba la 'dod pa'i don phun sum tshogs pa nges shing /_byang chub kyi lam la thams cad la khyab pas legs par gnas pa'o/_/dad pa'i nyid kyis zhes bya ba ni/_chos rnams dad pas gzung bya ste/_/shes rab ldan pa'i de nyid rig_/ces pa'o/_/
[\[40b.6\]]de nyid kyis na brtag pa'i rgyal po gnyis pa las gsungs pa/_lag pas ma yin dung por min/_/mkhas pas lce yi blang bya ste/_/tshe dang nad med 'phel byed pas/_/yang dag ye shes 'byung ba'i rgyu/_/mthe'u chung zhes bya ba mthe'u chung ni tshigs dang ldan pa ste/_lo bcu 
[\[40b.7\]]gcig pa'i bdag nyid bzung nas/_lce yis blangs te ra sa ya na'i dngos grub brtsam pa ni bde ba chen po 'byung ba'i rgyu'o/_/sgrub pa po ni gsum du gnas/_zhes bya ba ni chung ngu dang 'bring dang chen po'i bye brag gis so/_/dpal he ru ka zhes bya ba'i dka' 'grel sgrub pa'i 
[\[41a\]]
[\[41a.1\]]thabs kyi [(]gling [,]gleng [)]gzhi las le'u nyi shu rtsa drug pa'i rnam par bshad pa'o//_//de nas dpa' bo spyod brtul zhugs/_/zhes bya ba la kun la spyod pas na spyod pa'o/_/brtul zhugs ni rus pa'i rgyan la sogs pa'o/_/dpa' bo zhes bya ba ni rnal 'byor pa'o/_/de su 
[\[41a.2\]]zhig ce na/_rnal 'byor ma rnams rab bsgrub pa/_/zhes bya ba'o/_/mtshan nyid dbye ba shes byas nas/_/zhes bya ba la/_phung po khams sogs mdor bsdus nas/_/sgyu ma lta bur shes par bya/_/'jig rten chos brgyad yongs shes shing /_/dpal ldan slob dpon rdo rje can/_/
[\[41a.3\]]kun gyi mgon la phyag 'tshal nas/_/bya ba byas pa'i slob dpon gyis/_/gsang ba'i spyod pa bsten par bya/_/'khar gsil lhung bzed chos gos dang /_/chu tshags sbyangs pa'i yon tan gyis/_/yan lag lung bzhin mi spang ste/_/lung ma rtogs pa'i rnal 'byor pa/_/
[\[41a.4\]]sangs rgyas mu stegs can du 'gyur/_/shes rab pha rol phyin dmigs pa/_/lhag pa yi ni lha gcig nyid/_/stong pa'i ye shes yang dag 'byung /_/gdug pa thams cad 'jigs byed pa/_/sngon bzhin drag shul rnam 'gyur dpal/_/'jigs pa dag kyang 'jigs 
[\[41a.5\]]par mdzad/_/mche ba rab tu gtsigs pa po/_/gtsug na zla ba phyed pa la/_/sna tshogs rdo rje'i ral pas brgyan/_/thod pa'i phreng bas rnam par klubs/_/rgyan rnams thams cad yongs rdzogs shing /_/stag gi pags pas rnam dkris pa/_/phyed krung bcas pas shin tu gnas/_/
[\[41a.6\]]sdang ba thams cad 'dul mdzad pa/_/sred dang rmongs pa phrag dog 'gyur/_/kA la rA Ti 'og tu mnan/_/spyan gsum zhal bzhi mgrin pa bzhi/_/mkha' ltar sngo skya ser dang dmar/_/steng gi zhal ni ljang gu ste/_/rdo rje dril bus snyoms 'jug cing /_/phag mo dang ni gnyis med dga'/_/
[\[41a.7\]]chos kyi 'khor lo khar gsil snod/_/mdung dang zhags pa lcags kyu'i phyag_/de bzhin mgo dang rnge'u chung ste/_/phyag gnyis na ni chos gos so/_/mi lpags snam bu gnyis kyis 'phyar/_/phag mo mdog dmar gcer bu la/_/spyan gsum skra ni grol ba ste/_/rgyan rnams thams 
[\[41b\]]
[\[41b.1\]]cad rab 'bar ba/_/g.yon pa ro yi lhung bzed de/_/g.yas pa rnge'u chung chos dbyangs sgrogs/_/byin pa gnyis ni mnyam 'khyud cing /_/snying rje dga' ba'i bde ba che/_/shar la sogs pa'i 'dab ma la/_/rngon pa mo dang gdol pa ni/_/btso blag mkhan dang puk+ka sI/_/
[\[41b.2\]]shan pa ma dang ri khrod ma/_/de bzhin nya ba sha wa ri/_/ser dang sngo dmar dud kha dang /_/skya bo dang ni dmar dang ljang /_/de bzhin brgyad pa dkar po ste/_/lugs su 'byung dang lugs las bzlog_/bzhad dang zhi dang snying rje dang /_/sgeg dang rngam dang mi sdug pa/_/drag shul 
[\[41b.3\]]khro bo'i nyams dang ldan/_/khar gsil dang ni lhung bzed dang /_/rnge'u chung dang zangs gri phyag_/sgo bzhir khro mo dgod par bya/_/he ru ka yi rang snang ba/_/rdo rje gtum mo rdo rje'o/_/srin mo rdo rje 'jigs byed ma/_/drag shul gdul dka' kun gsol ma/_/thal ba'i 
[\[41b.4\]]mdog dang ljang gu dang /_/dmar dang sngo ba drag shul 'jigs/_/lhung bzed dang ni khar gsil g.yon/_/chos kyi 'khor lo rnge'u chung g.yas/_/phrag pa'i steng na chos gos 'phyar/_/kun kyang zhal gcig phyag bzhi pa/_/rgyan rnams kun gyis rnam par klubs/_/ro steng 
[\[41b.5\]]phyed krung gar gyis ni/_/bzhugs shing skra grol gcer bu'o/_/bsod snyoms pa dang chos gos gsum/_/phyis mi len dang cog pu pa/_/stan gcig pa dang bla gab med/_/shing drung pa dang dgon pa ba/_/dur khrod pa dang phyag dar khrod/_/phying pa can dang gzhi 
[\[41b.6\]]ji bzhin/_/sbyangs pa'i yon tan bcu gnyis po/_/de rnams rnam dag rnal 'byor pa/_/gsung rab yan lag bcu gnyis pa'i/_/chos kyi 'dul ba'i tshul khrims kyis/_/rab tu mgu zhing snying rje che/_/dga' ba'i rab tu mdzes pa'o/_/de rnams dang lhan cig spyod 
[\[41b.7\]]cing rjes su 'dug pa rnams dang bcas pa des yid dga' zhing /_rdo rje byin gyis brlab pa byas nas/_phyag rgya chen po'i bde bas bzlas pa bya ste/_oM shrI ma hA su kha badzra he he ru ru kaM huM hUM phaT phaT swA hA/_dam tshig gi kha zas la longs spyod cing /
[\[42a\]]
[\[42a.1\]]sangs rgyas kyi spyod pa dang /_brtul zhugs b+hu su ku'i spyod pa'i rim pa 'dis spyad par bya'o/_/de la rim pa ni 'di yin te b+hu zhes bya ba ni za ba ste/_de tsam 'ba' zhig rjes su dran zhing 'du 'dzi thams cad yongs su spangs nas/_dka' thub dang bsnyen gnas 
[\[42a.2\]]la sogs pa cung zad tsam yang bsam par mi bya'o/_/su zhig ces bya ba ni gnyid log pa ma rig pas nye bar mtshon pa 'di dag rnam par shes pa tsam du mngon du byas la dge ba'i rnam par bsnyen pa'o/_/de ci zhe na slar yang 'jug par bya ste/_rang bzhin gyis 'od gsal 
[\[42a.3\]]ba nyid du mngon du byas te/_rang bzhin gyis dri ma med pa'i phyir ro/_/kun zhes bya ba ni bshang bar 'gro ba ste/_bshang gci tsam zhig 'dor bar byed pa'o/_/des de tsam zhig rjes su bsams nas 'du 'dzi yongs su spangs nas/_lus dang tshor ba dang yul dang dbang

[\[55b\]]
[\[55b.1\]]par 'gyur ba'o/_/gang gi phyir brtson par ldan zhes bya ba ni dngos grub pa la rjes su chags pa ste/_ye shes dang ldan zhing nyams dga' ba rnam par 'byed pa de snyed nas/_dpal he ru ka mnyam par sbyor ba'i rnal 'byor pas shin tu chags pa'i dga' ba'i longs spyod bya'o/_/rnal 'byor 
[\[55b.2\]]rdzas kyi rnam 'byed yin/_/zhes bya ba la rdzas ni thams cad mkhyen pa'i ye shes kyi bdud rtsi ste bcud kyis len no/_/dper na lha'i shing gis 'dod pa'i don gyi 'bras bu ster ba de bzhin du 'dir yang byang chub kyi sems kyi shing gis sangs rgyas kyi 'bras bu ster ba'o/_/rnam 'byed bye brag 
[\[55b.3\]]gnyis su gsungs/_/spyod dang grol ba'i 'bras rab stsol/_/bla ma'i man ngag thob nas su/_/yang dag bsgom pa mngon par spro/_/rtag tu nus dang ldan pa yis/_/byang chub mchog ni bsgrub byas nas/_/tshe 'di nyid la sangs rgyas ni/_/rdo rje 'dzin pa chen por 'gyur/_/de'i don 
[\[55b.4\]]zhes bya ba ni gang gi tshe brtson 'grus dman par gyur pa rnams kyis/_bla na med pa'i ye shes ma thob pa de'i tshe 'dod pa'i don gyi longs spyod thob cing sbyin bdag dang mnyam par 'gyur ro zhes pa'o/_/dpal he ru ka zhes bya ba'i dka' 'grel sgrub pa'i thabs kyi gleng gzhi las le'u 
[\[55b.5\]]nyi shu rtsa brgyad pa'i rnam par bshad pa'o//_//de nas zhes bya ba ni gsungs ma thag pa'i rjes la'o/_/gzhan yang zhes bya ba ni khyad par gyi mtshan nyid do/_/bstan pa zhes bya ba ni sngon du'o/_/de nas gzhan yang shes par bya/_/zhes sbyar ro/_/gang gis zhes bya ba ni mtshan 
[\[55b.6\]]nyid kyis so/_/shes 'gyur zhes bya ba ni rnal 'byor can gyis so/_/dam tshig la gnas mkha' 'gro ma/_/zhes bya ba ni dam tshig las mi 'da' ba ste/_sgrub pa po rnams la phan pa'i phyir/_ma dang pha zhes bya ba la ma dang pha gang rnyed nas so/_/dang gi sgra ni lhan cig spyod pa'i rjes 
[\[55b.7\]]su 'jug pa'o/_/bdag po nyid du zhes bya ba ni ye shes thob par ro/_/skad cig mthong zhes bya ba ni bdag nyid de/_de dag gis de yongs su rdzogs pa ni so sor rang gis rig pa zhes bya ba'i bar du'o/_/de ltar yang gsungs pa/_pha las thob pa'i bde ba gang /_/bde de rang gis longs 
[\[56a\]]
[\[56a.1\]]spyad bya/_/bde ba gang gis 'dir 'chi ba/_/de yi bde ba bsam gtan brjod/_/ces so/_/lce sbom zhes bya ba la/_bzhin 'phel che zhing nu rgyas ma/_/bzhin yang thung zhing skra yang thung /_/glang chen dri bro dregs pa ma/_/mdor bsdus nas ni rab tu bshad/_/lus 
[\[56a.2\]]po ring zhing bzhin yang ring /_/de bzhin lag pa rkang pa ring /_/nya yi dri bro khu ba 'bab/_/skye gnas ring zhing zla ba 'dra/_/mnyam zhing sngo bsangs nu ma mkhrang /_/yan lag chung zhing skra yang srab/_/skye gnas chung zhing skra yang rtsub/_/dal bar smra zhing sna mtho ma/_/mgrin pa ha cang ring 
[\[56a.3\]]ba min/_/gzugs mdzes pad+ma dkar po 'dra/_/lus las rtag tu rdul 'byung ba/_/shin tu dri zhim yid 'phrog ma/_/mig dang lta stongs [{]ri dgas [,]ri dwags [}]'dra/_/[{]ri dgas [,]ri dwags [}]mgrin pa [{]ri dgas [,]ri dwags [}]lto/_/[{]ri dgas [,]ri dwags [}]rgyal po'i rked pa can/_/skal bzang pad+ma'i dri dang ldan/_/de yi mig ni dmar ser la/_/
[\[56a.4\]]zhes bya ba la dmar ser ni cung zad dmar ba ste/_de bzhin lag pa rkang pa'ang dmar/_/mA li ka yi dri 'byung zhing /_/bde gshegs 'dus la dga' ba yi/_/bud med dngos grub ster bar byed/_/lus rgyas nu ma yang rgyas shing /_/skra ni 'khyil bar gyur pa dang /_/ngang pa'i 
[\[56a.5\]]'gros can dal bar smra/_/shin tu ring min bzhin yang zlum/_/lte ba'i dbus su gnyer gsum ste/_/dbus nas 'phyang ba der shes bya/_/rtsub cing yan lag rtsub pa dang /_/lha sbyin lta bus gzhan la smod/_/skra ni 'khyil zhing kham par gnas/_/mdog ngan yan lag rtsub 
[\[56a.6\]]pa dang /_/skyes pa lta bur nu ma spangs/_/sA la lta bu'i dri yang bro/_/'od 'bar sA la'i dris byugs la/_/rtag tu chang la dga' ba ste/_/dam tshig spyod pa phun sum tshogs/_/bsam pa rtogs la nan tan byed/_/khyad par can gyi dri zhim pa/_/chang gi rjes su 'gro ba ste/_/
[\[56a.7\]]chang gi khyad par dag dang ni/_/chang gis shin tu myos pa dang /_/de bzhin khu ba mang po'o/_/dngos grub kun gyis thun mong du/_/zhes bya ba ni dge ba'i bshes gnyen gyis khyad par rnyed nas thar pa'i don du thams cad rjes su 'gro ba'o/_/dran zhes bya ba ni 'di'i mtshan nyid rnams 
[\[56b\]]
[\[56b.1\]]so/_/yang dag ldan zhes bya ba ni rnal 'byor ma'o/_/pho nya la sogs go 'phang bcas/_/zhes bya ba la go 'phang ni rab tu gnas zhes pa ste/_yon tan gyi gnas zhes bya ba yin la de dang bcas pa'o/_/gang gi yin zhe na/_mkha' 'gro ma mchog nyi shu bzhi/_/zhes bya ba ste de rnams 
[\[56b.2\]]kyis ma lus pa rnams bsdus pa'o/_/gang gi phyir de rnams kyis 'jig rten gsum du khyab pa zhes bya ba ste/_de dag rnyed nas mchod pa byas pas ni sgrub pa po la longs spyod dang grol ba'i 'bras bu rab tu stsol bar 'gyur ro/_/rtsa ba'i sngags ni rtag tu zlos/_/zhes bya ba 
[\[56b.3\]]la lam ngan pa thams cad las yid skyob pas na sngags te/_gnyis su med pa'i ye shes yin la/_de rnam par dag pa'i sngags zlos pa'o/_/'dod pa'i don kun sgrub byed pa/_/zhes bya ba la 'dod par bya bas 'dod pa ste/_sems can thams cad kyi don byed pa rdo rje 'dzin pa 
[\[56b.4\]]la sogs pa'i gzugs so/_/de'i sgrub pa po ste tshe 'di nyid la sangs rgyas nyid thob pa'i phyir ro/_/de nyid kyis na sngags 'di las byung ba yin te zhes bya ba ni/_shin tu goms pa rnyed par 'gyur ba'o zhes bya ba'i don no/_/de ci zhig ce na/_rgyu dang mi rgyu 'jig rten gsum/_/zhes bya ba 
[\[56b.5\]]ste/_'jig rten gsum ni yongs su dag pa'i sangs rgyas kyi zhing ngo /_/rgyu ba ni sems can gyi 'jig rten no/_/mi rgyu ba ni snod kyi 'jig rten no/_/de'i tshe de dag lhan cig rdo rje 'dzin pa'i ror ro gcig ces bya'o/_/yang na 'jig rten gsum ni sku rdo rje la sogs pa'o/_/de rnams kyi rgyu 
[\[56b.6\]]ba zhes bya ba ni byang chub kyi sems rdo rje ste/_bde ba 'dod pas skyed pa'i phyir rgyu'o/_/mi rgyu ba ni de thob pa ste de dag lhan cig 'jug pa zhes bya bar sbyar ro/_/de su'i zhe na sgrub pa po ni zhes bya ba ste/_gang gi phyir 'di sngags dang phyag rgya yang dag par ldan zhing dngos grub la brtson pa nyid 
[\[56b.7\]]do/_/de nyid kyis na/_/phyag rgya dang bral byin mi rlob/_/ces bya ba ste/_sku gsung thugs la 'debs pa ste/_/shes rab phyag rgyar rab tu brjod/_/de dang bral ba ni nyan thos ltar dad pa'i nor med pa'i phyir lus can de la sngags kyis byin gyis rlob par mi 'gyur ro/_/sngags zhes bya ba 
[\[57a\]]
[\[57a.1\]]ni/_gnyis su med pa'i ye shes te/_rdo rje 'dzin pa thob ces bya ba'i don no/_/gang gi phyir des na/_shin tu sbyar bas rtogs par bya/_/zhes bya ba ni/_yid gzhan du ma yengs pa ste shin tu brtson pas so/_/ji srid dro ba'i bya ba dag_/ces bya ba la/_dang po shes rab 
[\[57a.2\]]dro ba ste/_/gnyis pa du ba ldan par 'gyur/_/gsum pa la ni 'od 'khrug 'byung /_/bzhi pa mar me ltar 'bar ba/_/lnga pa rtag tu snang ba ni/_/sprin med pa yi nam mkha' bzhin/_/don 'di nyid gsal bar gsungs pa/_/snang ba cung zad gsal ba ni/_/dro bar gyur pa zhes 
[\[57a.3\]]bya'o/_/ye shes kyi snang ba de nyid 'bring por gsal ba ni rtse mor gyur pa zhes bya'o/_/gang gi tshe phyi rol gyi don gsal nas ye shes kyi snang ba mched nas rnam par rig pa tsam gyi gnas skabs su rtog pa'i tshogs las grol ba'i rgyun mi chad par 'od gsal ba ni bzod pa zhes bya'o/_/de nyid 
[\[57a.4\]]sangs rgyas kyi chos rnams mchog tu gyur pa ni chos kyi mchog ces bya ba'i don no/_/gang zhig sdang zhes bya ba la sogs pa ni rmongs shing sdig pa'i grogs po dang 'dus pas sun 'byin 'og tu phyogs pas mnar med par 'gro bar 'gyur ro/_/rnal 'byor che mchog ces bya ba la 
[\[57a.5\]]sogs pa la yul de yongs su brtags pa ste/_zhes bya ba ni ci nas kyang de'i rtsa lag rnams kyang mchod par bya'o zhes bya ba'o/_/de rung ni zhes bya ba ni rnal 'byor pa'i snying la gnas pa'o zhes bya ba'i don no/_/dpal he ru ka zhes bya ba'i dka' 'grel sgrub pa'i thabs kyi gleng gzhi las 
[\[57a.6\]]le'u nyi shu rtsa dgu pa'i rnam par bshad pa'o//_//de nas gzhan yang zhes bya ba ni/_he ru ka las gzhan pa ste/_/khro bo'i rgyal po gnod mdzes zhes bya ba'i don no/_/de'i mngon par rtogs pa'o/_/dgod pa ni dbyangs dang yi ge rnams [{]go rims [,]go rim [}]ji lta ba bzhin rgyas par byas 
[\[57a.7\]]pa'o/_/sa'i cha ni zhes bya ba la sogs pa ni 'byung po thams cad pa'i gtor ma byin pa'o/_/kun du 'gyur ba'i sa bon ni/_/yi ge hUM gi cha gzugs can/_/mi 'gyur ba ni bde ba ste/_/nam mkha' dang mnyam gnas med pa/_/de nas go cha bcings byas nas/_/thams 
[\[57b\]]
[\[57b.1\]]cad stong pa rten med pa/_/rdza rnga zhes bya'i yi ge gsum/_/sku gsung thugs kyi dbye bas sku gsum mo/_/rlung gis shin tu bskor ba yi/_/ri mo brgyad la yang dag brten/_/ri mo dgu pa dbus gnas pa/_/stong pa'i rnam pa dri med dgod/_/A li kA li mnyam rgyas bya/_/dga' 
[\[57b.2\]]ba bzhi yi rang bzhin can/_/chos kyi rnga chen mi shigs pa/_/gsung rab yan lag bcu gnyis pa/_/chos kyi yi ge yongs rdzogs pa/_/de nas grangs kyi nang song bas/_/chos bdag med pa yang dag bsten/_/zhal bzhi par ni rnam par grags/_/bdud bzhi rnam par 'joms 
[\[57b.3\]]pa'o/_/rlung la zhon nas spro byed cing /_/khams gsum pa ni yang dag sdud/_/rlung gi de nyid shin tu rig_/rim bzhin 'byung dang ri ma min pa'i/_/A li kA li las byung ba/_/rjod dang brjod bya'i yi ge ste/_/sgra brnyan lta bu'i rnam par rtog_/re mig sum cu gnyis pa blang /_/
[\[57b.4\]]zhes bya ba la sogs pa ni ji lta ba bzhin te go sla'o/_/oM sum+b+ha ni sum+b+ha huM hUM phaT/_oM gr-ih+Na gr-ih+Na huM hUM phaT/_oM gr-ih+NA pa ya gr-ih+NA pa ya huM hUM phaT/_oM A na ya ho b+ha ga bAn badzra rA dza huM hUM phaT/_'di ni khams gsum rnam rgyal zhes bya ba'o/_/sngags tshig bzhi yis rnam par brgyan/_/
[\[57b.5\]]dkyil 'khor bsrung ba'i don du ni/_/gdug pa thams cad nges gnon pas/_/khro bo bzhi dang yang dag ldan/_/skad cig gis ni sku brgyad 'gyur/_/zhes bya ba la sngags re re zhing sku gnyis gnyis spro bar bya ste/_shin tu drag cing bgegs thams cad sel bar byed pa'i 'khor lo 
[\[57b.6\]]chen po'i khor yug gi mthar thug par khwa'i gdong pa la sogs pa brgyad/_zhal gcig pa phyag gnyis pa la zangs gri dang kha T+wAM ga 'dzin pa 'od zer dmar po 'bar ba'i spyan gsum pa drag cing 'jigs par byed pa lang tsho la bab pa/_gzhon zhing rnam par 'gyur ba'i zhal can 
[\[57b.7\]]stag gi pags pas rked pa dkris pa smad kyi cha phur bu'i rnam par rno zhing 'bar la bskal pa 'jig pa'i me dang 'dra bar 'od zer 'bar ba la gnas pa'o/_/de nas phyogs mtshams brgyad du bgegs kyi tshogs rnams dong gi nang du chud par byas la lugs su 'byung ba dang 
[\[58a\]]
[\[58a.1\]]lugs las bzlog bas khro bo'i phur bu rnams sngags 'dis dgod par bya ste/_oM g+ha g+ha g+hA ta ya g+hA ta ya sarba duSh+TAM hUM phaT/_kI la ya kI la ya sarba pA paM hUM phaT/_hUM hUM hUM badzra kI la ya badzra d+har A dz+nyA pa ya ti kA ya bA ka tsit+ta badzra kI la ya hUM phaT khro bo'i lta 
[\[58a.2\]]bas nges par bltas la/_rdo rje'i tho bas brdung bar bya'o/_/oM badzra mung+ga ra badzra kI la ya/_A ko Ta ya hUM phaT/_yi ge b+h+rUM gyis bskyed pa'i 'khor lo la rdo rje gtum po dang /_rdo rje nyi ma dang /_rdo rje gtsug tor dang /_rdo rje 'khyil pa dang /_rdo rje gnod sbyin dang /_rdo rje nag po dang /_rdo rje 
[\[58a.3\]]stobs po che dang /_rdo rje 'jigs byed dang /_de bzhin du gtsug tor 'khor los sgyur ba dang /_'og tu gnod mdzes rab tu grags/_sdug bsngal ba dang kun 'byung ba dang 'gog pa dang lam dang zad pa dang /_mi skye ba dang /_chos dang /_rjes su shes pa dang /_kun rdzob dang /_sgra ji 
[\[58a.4\]]bzhin pa'i ye shes rnam par dag pa la khro bo bcu spro bar bya'o/_/dbus su ni rdo rje sems dpa' yongs su gyur pa las bdag nyid rdo rje hUM mdzad du bskyed par bya ste/_mdog sngo zhing drag shul che ba/_gzi brjid che zhing mche ba gtsigs pa dang bcas shing 'jigs par byed pa 
[\[58a.5\]]zhal g.yas dang g.yon pa ser po dang ljang gu dpa' bo dang mi sdug pa ljags 'dril ba'i zhal dang smin ma bsnyer ba'i khro gnyer gyis gtsigs pa ste/_spyan ni dmar zhing zlum pa ste/_/khro bo 'bar ba'i phreng 'khrug drag_/rdo rje hUM yig hUM mdzad pa/_/mi rus rgyan gyis rnam par bklubs/_/
[\[58a.6\]]mgo yi me tog phreng ba can/_/sangs rgyas lnga ni spro ba'i bdag_/phyag gnyis rdo rje dril bu dang /_/'jig rten gsum rgyal phyag rgya bcings/_/shes rab dang ni snyoms 'jug pa/_/lhag ma'i phyag gis lcags kyu zhags/_/ral gri thod pa kha T+wAM 'dzin/_/g.yas brkyang ba yis 
[\[58a.7\]]gdan la bzhugs/_/'jigs byed dang ni dus mtshan dag_/zhabs kyi pad+ma'i 'og tu byas/_/de'i shar gyi rtsibs la rdo rje gtum po ste/_mdog sngo zhing g.yas pa dang /_g.yon pa'i zhal ser po dang ljang gu/_phyag g.yas na rdo rje dang tho ba dang sdigs mdzub po/_/g.yon na thod 
[\[58b\]]
[\[58b.1\]]dang kha T+wAM ga phyag gnyis kyis rang dang 'dra ba'i shes rab la 'khyud pa'o/_/byang phyogs su ni nyi ma ste/_mdog ser zhing zhal g.yas pa dang g.yon pa dag sngon po dang ljang gu rang dang 'dra ba'i shes rab la 'khyud cing /_rdo rje dang dbyug pa dang thod pa dang kha T+wAM ga 'dzin pa'o/_/nub phyogs su 
[\[58b.2\]]rdo rje gtsug tor te mdog dmar zhing g.yas pa dang g.yon pa dag dkar po dang ljang gu rang dang 'dra ba'i shes rab la 'khyud cing pad+ma dmar po dang thod pa dang kha T+wAM ga 'dzin pa'o/_/rdo rje 'khyil pa ni lho phyogs su ste/_mdog ljang gu rang dang 'dra ba'i shes rab la 'khyud cing g.yas pa dang 
[\[58b.3\]]g.yon pa dag ser po dang dkar po/_sna tshogs rdo rje thod pa dang kha T+wAM ga 'dzin pa'o/_/me'i mtshams su rdo rje gnod sbyin du ba'i mdog can g.yas pa dang g.yon pa dag ser po dang ljang gu mdzes shing rang gi shes rab ma la 'khyud pa dang /_mdung rtse gsum pa dang /_thod pa dang kha T+wAM ga 
[\[58b.4\]]'dzin pa'o/_/bden bral du ni rdo rje nag po mdog dmar zhing g.yas pa dang g.yon pa dag ser po dang ljang gu rang gi shes rab dang snyoms par zhugs pa dang /_dgra sta dang thod pa dang kha T+wAM ga 'dzin pa'o/_/rlung mtshams su ni rdo rje stobs po che mdog sngo zhing g.yas pa dang g.yon pa ser po dang 
[\[58b.5\]]ljang gu ste rang gi shes rab la snyoms par zhugs pa dang /_mdung rtse gsum pa dang thod pa dang kha T+wAM ga 'dzin pa'o/_/dbang ldan du ni rdo rje 'jigs byed mdog sngo zhing g.yas pa dang g.yon pa dag ser po dang ljang gu'i mdog rang gi shes rab ma la 'khyud cing ral gri dang thod pa dang kha 
[\[58b.6\]]T+wAM ga 'dzin pa'o/_/steng du gtsug tor 'khor los sgyur ba ste/_mdog dkar zhing g.yas pa dang g.yon pa dag sngon po dang ljang gu rang gi shes rab la 'khyud cing 'khor lo dang thod pa dang kha T+wAM ga 'dzin pa'o/_/'og tu ni gnod mdzes rdo rje ste/_sngo zhing g.yas pa dang g.yon pa 
[\[58b.7\]]dag ser po dang ljang gu rang gi shes rab la 'khyud cing /_rdo rje gtun shing dang klu'i zhags pa thogs nas sdigs mdzub byed pa'o/_/khro bo thams cad kyang sna tshogs pad+ma dang nyi ma'i dbus su gyur pa'i 'jig rten skyong ba brgyad g.yas brkyang bas mnan nas bzhugs pa ste/
[\[59a\]]
[\[59a.1\]]'phags skyes po dang /_mig mi bzang dang /_yul 'khor srung dang /_rnam thos kyi bu dang /_de bzhin du me dang /_srin po dang /_rlung bdag dang dbang ldan gyi lha'o/_/steng du ni lha'i bdag po tshangs pa'o/_/'og tu ni thags [{]bzangs [,]bzang [}]ris te/_de dag gi snying gar 
[\[59a.2\]]lugs su 'byung ba dang lugs las bzlog pa dgod do/_/khro bo thams cad kyang skra kham pa gyen du 'bar zhing smin ma bsnyer ba'i khro gnyer rnam par gtsigs pa'i zhal can thung zhing gsus pa 'phyang ba spyan gsum pa dmar zhing zlum pa mgo bo'i phreng bas do shal byas shing mi rus 
[\[59a.3\]]kyi rgyan gyis bklubs pa/_mtha' yas sngon po'i sbrul gyi spyi gtsug gi nor bu byas shing /_'jog po dmar po'i 'od bsgyings pas rna cha byas pa'o/_/pad+ma dkar po do shal byas pas brgyan zhing /_stobs kyi rgyu ljang gus ni mchod phyir thogs byas pa'o/_/dung skyong ser pos 
[\[59a.4\]]ni lag gdub byas pa'o/_/rigs ldan sna tshogs kyi mchog can ni dpung rgyan du yang dag par btags pa'o/_/pad+ma dkar pos ske rags su byas pa'o/_/nor rgyas kyi bu mdog pad+ma'i rtsa dang 'dra bas rkang gdub byas pa ste/_skad cig tsam gyis sku dang gsung dang thugs brgyan 
[\[59a.5\]]zhing /_byang chub kyi sems kyi chus dbang bskur bas rang gi rigs las byung ba'i dbu rgyan can rdo rje 'bar ba'i 'od 'khrug cing drag po rang gi lus las rnam par spro ba nam mkha'i dkyil 'khor g.yogs pa/_phyogs bcu'i mthar thug par khro bo 'bar ba'i rigs kyis 'khrug 
[\[59a.6\]]pa'o/_/de nas/_bka' yi 'khor lo myur 'khor ba/_/de nyid rig pas g.yon du bskor/_/lha rnams sems kyis brtan byas nas/_/de nas bgegs ni med par 'gyur/_/shin tu rnam dag de bzhin gshegs/_/'di ni rnyed pa tsam gyis su/_/gdug pa thams cad 'chi bar 
[\[59a.7\]]'gyur/_/nyon mongs bdud dgra 'joms par byed/_/gang dag 'di las 'da' byed na/_/brlag 'gyur 'di la the tshom med/_/dpal ldan rdo rje hUM mdzad kyi/_/'khor lor sbyor bdag rdza rngar bcas/_/bstan pa'i las rnams brtsam byas nas/_/dngos grub kun gyi 'bras 'byung 
[\[59b\]]
[\[59b.1\]]ba'o/_/dpal he ru ka zhes bya ba'i dka' 'grel sgrub pa'i thabs kyi gleng gzhi las le'u sum cu pa'i rnam par bshad pa'o//_//de nas sha chen thams cad kyi/_/zhes bya ba la sogs pa la thams cad ni zhe sdang la sogs pa nyon mongs pa rnams te/_dam tshig rnams kyi mthus de rnams 
[\[59b.2\]]'jigs par 'gyur ba'o/_/des cir 'gyur zhe na/_rdo rje skyes zhes bya ba ni rdo rje ni dbyer med pa'i ye shes so/_/de las skyes pa ni rdo rje 'dzin pa'i sku'o/_/'di ni zhes bya ba ni 'di rnams te/_gdug pa thams cad kyis zhes bya ba ni zhe sdang la sogs pa'i 'jigs byed mi bzad pa'o/_/rdo rje skyes 
[\[59b.3\]]zhes bya ba'i nye bar bsdus pa'o/_/'di ltar khyi dang zhes bya ba la sogs pa bza' ba yang yin la sbyin sreg kyang yin pas na/_bza' ba sbyin sreg gtor ma bya/_/zhes so/_/rtog dang bral bas bya zhes bya ba ni rang gi rang bzhin gyis 'od gsal ba'i phyir ro/_/de yang kha na ma tho ba med pa 
[\[59b.4\]]rnyed nas/_rdo rje'i rnal 'byor can gyis bya ba ste/_gzhan du na sems can la gnod pa byas par 'gyur ro/_/de yang gsungs pa/_sems can gnod pa tsam gyis ni/_/phyag rgya'i dngos grub thob mi 'gyur/_/rnam par mi rtog sems gang zhig_/thab khung sgra ni rab tu grag_/rnam 
[\[59b.5\]]dben me ni rab sbar la/_/ma lus rnam par rtog pa yis/_/sha dang sha chen sbyin sreg bya/_/skye bos smad pa gang ci'ang rung /_/srog chags yan lag las byung dang /_/sna tshogs chang ni btung bar bya/_/zhes bya ba'o/_/ma lus pa ni 'jig rten gsum po'o/_/de dang de rnams 
[\[59b.6\]]su gang bcom ldan 'das kyis gsungs pa de ni lus pa med pa ste dngos grub ces bya ba'i don no/_/rgyal srid thams cad ces bya ba ni rdo rje 'dzin pa nyid do/_/dung dang nya phyis mu tig rnams/_/gsum char rgyu las byung yin na/_/zhes bya ba la sogs pa la rgyu ni las mi dge ba

[\[77b\]]
[\[77b.1\]]gzhan yang bshad bya ba/_/zhes bya ba 'di ni rab tu dga' ba la sogs pa'o/_/dpal he ru ka zhes bya ba'i dka' 'grel sgrub thabs kyi gleng gzhi las le'u lnga bcu pa'i rnam par bshad pa'o//_//de nas gzhan yang bshad bya ba/_/gzhung lugs shin tu rnyed dka' ba/_/zhes bya ba ni 'dir yang phyag na rdo rje la bos 
[\[77b.2\]]te gsungs pa'o/_/sangs rgyas thams cad kyi man ngag bla ma chen po la gus pa ma gtogs par dka' bas kyang rnyed par mi 'gyur bas shin tu yang rnyed dka' ste/_chos kyi de kho na nyid ji ltar 'gyur bas na/_bden pa gnyis la legs par gnas pa'i zung du 'jug pa rgyu ba ste/_phyag rgya chen pos de mngon par rdzogs pa'i 
[\[77b.3\]]sangs rgyas byang chub pa'o/_/de yang rim pa ji lta bzhin/_/nyams pa med par lhag par khong du chud cing bsgrub par bya ba'i rjes su 'jug pa sgrub pa po rnams kyi sgrub thabs dang spyod pa'i cho ga shin tu gsal ba bskyed pa'i rim pa nas brtsams nas/_ji srid rdzogs pa'i rim pa'i mthar thug pa'i ting nge 'dzin mngon sum du

[\[77b.5\]]mchog tu gyur pa dang /_/gcig nas gcig brgyud man ngag ste/_/thos dang bsam pa sgom pa yis/_/shes rab ye shes goms pa ni/_/rnal 'byor rgyun chags rab 'brel pas/_/rgyun mi chad don skal ba can/_/yongs su smin cing yongs su byang /_/snod ldan de yi phyir bstan pas/_/'ong ba gang yin 'dir gzhung lugs/_/rgyud 
[\[77b.6\]]rnams kun du gsungs pa gang /_/gsang chen sangs rgyas kun gnas pa/_/'bum phrag sum 'gyur gang yin nyid/_/rab rgyas rgyud la 'ongs pa'o/_/des na bskyed pa'i rim pa la brten pa'i de kho na nyid bcu bzhi sgom pa'i rdzogs pa'i rim pa mthar thug pa'i nyon mongs pa dang bral ba'am brtson 'grus dang ldan pas dpa' bo ste dge

[\[78a\]]
[\[78a.1\]]lnga ni 'khor lo lnga la sogs pa'o/_/yang na me long lta bu'i ye shes la sogs pa'i ye shes lnga'o/_/shes rab yan lag ces bya ba ni kha T+wAM ga'o/_/yang na shes rab kyis mgrin par 'khyud pa'o/_/phur bu 'dra zhes bya ba ni khro bo'i phur bu dang rdo rje'i ra ba dang bla re dang mda'i dra ba dang me long gi dra ba la sogs pa'o/_/A li kA li

[\[78a.3\]]shes so/_/'jug pa sgra la sogs pa nas/_/zhes bya ba la rgyu rdo rje 'dzin pa ni 'jug pa'o/_/'jug pa'i rgyu ni sgra ste gnas skabs bzhi pa'i yi ge hUM ngo /_/sogs pa zhes bya ba ni zla ba dang nyi ma dang mtshan ma'i bar du'o/_/ji srid bsdus pa'i sbyor ldan pa/_/zhes bya ba ni gsum po de dag gong bu'i gzugs su bsdus pa'i sbyor ba ste 
[\[78a.4\]]bya ba grub pa'i rnal 'byor ro/_/bdud rtsis tshim byed ces bya ba shin tu rnam par dag pa'i ye shes kyis rdo rje 'dzin pa yongs su brnyes pa'o/_/mya ngan 'da' zhes bya ba ni nye bar bsdus pa'o/_/lag pa'i mchod pa zhes bya ba ni bshad zin to/_/'di lta bur gyur pa yang /_gal te bag med pas ma grub na/_de 'pho ba'i tshe rnal 'byor pa rnams zhes bya ba 
[\[78a.5\]]la sogs pa gsungs te go sla'o/_/dpal he ru ka zhes bya ba'i dka' 'grel sgrub thabs kyi gleng gzhi las le'u lnga bcu rtsa gcig pa'i rnam par bshad pa'o//_//sna tshogs bla ma'i zhal snga nas kyi bka' drin dang /_/slob dpon du ma'i gzhung lugs rnam par bltas pa yis/_/bdag nyid dran par bya phyir sgrub thabs 
[\[78a.6\]]gleng gzhi ni/_/mchog tu gsal zhing 'bar ba dad pas bris pa yin/_/bdag gis rnal 'byor mi gnyis kyi/_/dka' 'grel rdzogs par byas pa las/_/gyur pa'i dge ba gang yin des/_/rnal 'byor can gyi don byed shog_/dpal 'khor lo sdom pa'i dka' 'grel sgrub thabs kyi gleng gzhi zhes bya ba/_slob dpon chen po la
